serviceTranslations / cmr9d1kil0005vcju88srqwff EN

Update only the following fields; preserve the rest of this row.

seoTitle

Before
Skin Consultation Online | Romania | English Doctor | Same Day
After
Initial skin assessment | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Assessment for rash, eczema, acne, and other skin concerns. Our CMR-registered doctors assess skin conditions by secure video call in English. Same-day appointments available anywhere in Romania.
After
Initial skin assessment. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Skin Consultation — Online Assessment in English
After
Initial skin assessment

detailBody

Before
<p>Skin conditions are among the most visible and most undertreated medical concerns. Whether it is a rash that appeared this morning, an eczema flare that has been building for weeks, acne that is affecting your confidence, or a lesion you have been meaning to have checked — most skin concerns can be assessed clinically by video call by an experienced doctor.</p><p>Accessing a dermatologist in Romania takes time — even in major cities, private dermatology waiting times are typically several weeks for non-urgent conditions. For common skin conditions, GP-level assessment covers the majority of clinical need without requiring specialist dermatology input. Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer comprehensive skin assessments by secure video call — identifying your condition, advising on evidence-based management, and coordinating dermatology referral where needed. Same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adults and children presenting with:</p><ul><li>A new rash, skin change, or lesion that needs clinical assessment</li><li>An existing skin condition that is flaring or not responding to current treatment</li><li>Acne — from mild to moderate presentations requiring structured medical management</li><li>Eczema or atopic dermatitis — flare assessment and ongoing management</li><li>Psoriasis — assessment and management at GP level</li><li>A mole or skin lesion that has changed or is causing concern</li><li>Allergic skin reactions — urticaria, contact dermatitis, or drug reactions</li><li>Scalp conditions — seborrhoeic dermatitis, scalp psoriasis, or fungal conditions</li><li>Skin infections — bacterial, viral, or fungal</li><li>Chronic skin conditions requiring ongoing GP-level management</li><li>Patients who need a dermatology referral letter</li></ul><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Inflammatory conditions</li><li>Eczema and atopic dermatitis — flare management, trigger identification, ongoing care</li><li>Psoriasis — plaque, guttate, and scalp presentations at GP level</li><li>Seborrhoeic dermatitis — scalp and facial presentation</li><li>Rosacea — assessment and management</li><li>Perioral dermatitis</li><li>Acne</li><li>Mild to moderate acne — comedonal, inflammatory, and mixed</li><li>Acne in teenagers and adults</li><li>Hormonal acne in women — assessed alongside hormonal assessment where indicated</li><li>Post-acne pigmentation and scarring assessment</li><li>Acne management review — patients already on treatment who need clinical review</li></ul><p>Note: Isotretinoin for severe acne requires dermatologist assessment and supervision in Romania and cannot be initiated through this service. Your doctor advises on dermatology referral if your presentation warrants isotretinoin consideration.</p><ul><li>Allergic and reactive conditions</li><li>Urticaria — acute and chronic hives</li><li>Allergic contact dermatitis — assessment and allergen avoidance advice</li><li>Drug reactions affecting the skin</li><li>Angioedema — assessment and management at GP level</li><li>Food allergy-related skin presentations</li><li>Infections</li><li>Impetigo — bacterial skin infection</li><li>Cellulitis — assessment and management with escalation where indicated</li><li>Folliculitis</li><li>Fungal skin infections — tinea corporis, tinea pedis, tinea versicolor</li><li>Molluscum contagiosum</li><li>Warts and verrucae</li><li>Herpes simplex — cold sores and cutaneous herpes</li><li>Shingles — assessment and management</li><li>Pigmentation and lesions</li><li>Moles and skin lesions that have changed in size, shape, colour, or texture</li><li>Post-inflammatory hyperpigmentation</li><li>Melasma — assessment and management</li><li>Vitiligo — assessment and referral where indicated</li><li>Scalp conditions</li><li>Seborrhoeic dermatitis of the scalp</li><li>Scalp psoriasis</li><li>Scalp folliculitis</li><li>Tinea capitis — fungal scalp infection</li><li>Paediatric skin conditions</li><li>Eczema in infants and children</li><li>Nappy rash and infant skin concerns</li><li>Childhood viral rashes — assessment and guidance for parents</li><li>Impetigo and skin infections in children</li><li>Molluscum contagiosum in children</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Visual clinical assessment Skin conditions are uniquely suited to video consultation — your doctor can observe the lesion, rash, or skin change directly during the call and conduct a structured clinical assessment. Good lighting and a clear camera view significantly improve the quality of assessment — your doctor guides you on positioning during the call.</p><p>Comprehensive history Your doctor reviews the onset, progression, distribution, associated symptoms, triggers, previous treatments, relevant medical history, medications, and family history of skin conditions.</p><p>Diagnosis and management plan Based on the assessment, your doctor advises on the most likely diagnosis, the appropriate management approach, and what to expect from treatment — including how to monitor your condition and when to seek further review.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriate When clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><p>Investigation requests where indicated Where blood tests — including allergy panels or inflammatory markers — are clinically indicated, your doctor issues the request on the same day.</p><p>Dermatology referral where needed Where specialist dermatology assessment is required — including suspicious lesions, severe or treatment-resistant conditions, or conditions requiring biopsy or patch testing — your doctor issues a formal referral letter and advises on urgency.</p><h2>Skin Lesions — When to Act Promptly</h2><p>Any change in a mole or skin lesion warrants clinical assessment. The ABCDE criteria are the standard clinical guide:</p><ul><li>A — Asymmetry — one half does not match the other</li><li>B — Border — irregular, ragged, or blurred edges</li><li>C — Colour — variation in colour within the lesion</li><li>D — Diameter — larger than 6mm</li><li>E — Evolution — any change in size, shape, colour, or any new symptom</li></ul><p>If your lesion meets any of these criteria, book an assessment promptly. Your doctor assesses visually and advises on whether urgent in-person dermatology assessment is needed.</p><p>Skin cancer diagnosis requires in-person dermoscopy and biopsy where indicated. Video consultation allows clinical assessment and urgent referral — it does not replace in-person specialist assessment for suspicious lesions.</p><h2>Preparing for Your Skin Consultation</h2><p>Getting the most from a video-based skin assessment requires a little preparation:</p><p>Good lighting — natural daylight or a lamp directed at the affected area gives your doctor the clearest view</p><p>Close-up photos — take clear photos of the affected area before your consultation — these can be shared during the call and help your doctor assess changes over time</p><ul><li>Multiple angles — show the lesion or rash from multiple angles during the call</li></ul><p>Note the history — when it started, how it has changed, what makes it better or worse, and what you have already tried</p><h2>Skin Conditions and Romania — What to Expect</h2><p>Dermatology in Romania is well-developed in the private sector — particularly in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. However, English-speaking dermatologists are limited even in major cities, and waiting times at private dermatology clinics are typically several weeks for non-urgent conditions.</p><p>For common skin conditions — eczema, acne, rashes, and infections — GP-level assessment covers the majority of clinical need. Our service provides this assessment same day, from anywhere in Romania, in English, Romanian. When specialist dermatology input is genuinely needed, your doctor issues a formal referral letter on the same day.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Can a skin condition really be assessed by video call? Yes — for the vast majority of presentations. Dermatology is one of the medical specialties most suited to visual assessment and a video consultation allows your doctor to observe the condition directly. Where a condition cannot be adequately assessed remotely — suspicious lesions requiring dermoscopy, conditions requiring biopsy or patch testing — your doctor advises clearly and coordinates in-person referral.</p><p>Can I get acne treatment through this service? Yes — for mild to moderate acne. Your doctor conducts a full assessment and advises on the most appropriate management approach. Isotretinoin requires dermatologist assessment and supervision in Romania and cannot be initiated through this service. If your acne warrants isotretinoin consideration, your doctor advises on dermatology referral.</p><p>I have a mole that has changed — how urgent is this? Changed moles warrant prompt clinical assessment. Book a consultation and show your doctor the lesion during the video call. Your doctor assesses using standard clinical criteria and advises on whether urgent in-person dermatology assessment is needed.</p><p>Can I use this service for my child's eczema? Yes. Eczema in infants and children is one of the most common paediatric skin presentations and is a core part of this service.</p><p>Can I get a dermatology referral through this service? Yes. Where specialist dermatology input is clinically indicated, your doctor issues a formal referral letter on the same day — addressed to a dermatologist or clinic of your choice.</p><p>What if my rash is spreading rapidly or I feel unwell alongside it? A rapidly spreading rash accompanied by fever, difficulty breathing, facial swelling, or feeling seriously unwell is a medical emergency. Call 112 or go to your nearest emergency department immediately — do not book a video consultation.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance? No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions issued through this service are dispensed at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p><p>I am not in Bucharest — can I still use this service? Yes. This service is available from anywhere in Romania. All you need is a stable internet connection and a device with a camera.</p>
After
<p>Skin conditions are among the most visible and most undertreated medical concerns. Whether it is a rash that appeared this morning, an eczema flare that has been building for weeks, acne that is affecting your confidence, or a lesion you have been meaning to have checked — most skin concerns can be assessed clinically by video call by an experienced doctor.</p><p>Accessing a dermatologist in Romania takes time — even in major cities, private dermatology waiting times are typically several weeks for non-urgent conditions. For common skin conditions, GP-level assessment covers the majority of clinical need without requiring specialist dermatology input. Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer comprehensive skin assessments by secure video call — identifying your condition, advising on evidence-based management, and coordinating dermatology referral where needed. Same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adults and children presenting with:</p><ul><li>A new rash, skin change, or lesion that needs clinical assessment</li><li>An existing skin condition that is flaring or not responding to current treatment</li><li>Acne — from mild to moderate presentations requiring structured medical management</li><li>Eczema or atopic dermatitis — flare assessment and ongoing management</li><li>Psoriasis — assessment and management at GP level</li><li>A mole or skin lesion that has changed or is causing concern</li><li>Allergic skin reactions — urticaria, contact dermatitis, or drug reactions</li><li>Scalp conditions — seborrhoeic dermatitis, scalp psoriasis, or fungal conditions</li><li>Skin infections — bacterial, viral, or fungal</li><li>Chronic skin conditions requiring ongoing GP-level management</li><li>Patients who need a dermatology referral letter</li></ul><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Inflammatory conditions</li><li>Eczema and atopic dermatitis — flare management, trigger identification, ongoing care</li><li>Psoriasis — plaque, guttate, and scalp presentations at GP level</li><li>Seborrhoeic dermatitis — scalp and facial presentation</li><li>Rosacea — assessment and management</li><li>Perioral dermatitis</li><li>Acne</li><li>Mild to moderate acne — comedonal, inflammatory, and mixed</li><li>Acne in teenagers and adults</li><li>Hormonal acne in women — assessed alongside hormonal assessment where indicated</li><li>Post-acne pigmentation and scarring assessment</li><li>Acne management review — patients already on treatment who need clinical review</li></ul><p>Note: Isotretinoin for severe acne requires dermatologist assessment and supervision in Romania and cannot be initiated through this service. Your doctor advises on dermatology referral if your presentation warrants isotretinoin consideration.</p><ul><li>Allergic and reactive conditions</li><li>Urticaria — acute and chronic hives</li><li>Allergic contact dermatitis — assessment and allergen avoidance advice</li><li>Drug reactions affecting the skin</li><li>Angioedema — assessment and management at GP level</li><li>Food allergy-related skin presentations</li><li>Infections</li><li>Impetigo — bacterial skin infection</li><li>Cellulitis — assessment and management with escalation where indicated</li><li>Folliculitis</li><li>Fungal skin infections — tinea corporis, tinea pedis, tinea versicolor</li><li>Molluscum contagiosum</li><li>Warts and verrucae</li><li>Herpes simplex — cold sores and cutaneous herpes</li><li>Shingles — assessment and management</li><li>Pigmentation and lesions</li><li>Moles and skin lesions that have changed in size, shape, colour, or texture</li><li>Post-inflammatory hyperpigmentation</li><li>Melasma — assessment and management</li><li>Vitiligo — assessment and referral where indicated</li><li>Scalp conditions</li><li>Seborrhoeic dermatitis of the scalp</li><li>Scalp psoriasis</li><li>Scalp folliculitis</li><li>Tinea capitis — fungal scalp infection</li><li>Paediatric skin conditions</li><li>Eczema in infants and children</li><li>Nappy rash and infant skin concerns</li><li>Childhood viral rashes — assessment and guidance for parents</li><li>Impetigo and skin infections in children</li><li>Molluscum contagiosum in children</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Visual clinical assessment Skin conditions are uniquely suited to video consultation — your doctor can observe the lesion, rash, or skin change directly during the call and conduct a structured clinical assessment. Good lighting and a clear camera view significantly improve the quality of assessment — your doctor guides you on positioning during the call.</p><p>Comprehensive history Your doctor reviews the onset, progression, distribution, associated symptoms, triggers, previous treatments, relevant medical history, medications, and family history of skin conditions.</p><p>Diagnosis and management plan Based on the assessment, your doctor advises on the most likely diagnosis, the appropriate management approach, and what to expect from treatment — including how to monitor your condition and when to seek further review.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriate When clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><p>Investigation requests where indicated Where blood tests — including allergy panels or inflammatory markers — are clinically indicated, your doctor issues the request on the same day.</p><p>Dermatology referral where needed Where specialist dermatology assessment is required — including suspicious lesions, severe or treatment-resistant conditions, or conditions requiring biopsy or patch testing — your doctor issues a formal referral letter and advises on urgency.</p><h2>Skin Lesions — When to Act Promptly</h2><p>Any change in a mole or skin lesion warrants clinical assessment. The ABCDE criteria are the standard clinical guide:</p><ul><li>A — Asymmetry — one half does not match the other</li><li>B — Border — irregular, ragged, or blurred edges</li><li>C — Colour — variation in colour within the lesion</li><li>D — Diameter — larger than 6mm</li><li>E — Evolution — any change in size, shape, colour, or any new symptom</li></ul><p>If your lesion meets any of these criteria, book an assessment promptly. Your doctor assesses visually and advises on whether urgent in-person dermatology assessment is needed.</p><p>Skin cancer diagnosis requires in-person dermoscopy and biopsy where indicated. Video consultation allows clinical assessment and urgent referral — it does not replace in-person specialist assessment for suspicious lesions.</p><h2>Preparing for Your Skin Consultation</h2><p>Getting the most from a video-based skin assessment requires a little preparation:</p><p>Good lighting — natural daylight or a lamp directed at the affected area gives your doctor the clearest view</p><p>Close-up photos — take clear photos of the affected area before your consultation — these can be shared during the call and help your doctor assess changes over time</p><ul><li>Multiple angles — show the lesion or rash from multiple angles during the call</li></ul><p>Note the history — when it started, how it has changed, what makes it better or worse, and what you have already tried</p><h2>Skin Conditions and Romania — What to Expect</h2><p>Dermatology in Romania is well-developed in the private sector — particularly in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. However, English-speaking dermatologists are limited even in major cities, and waiting times at private dermatology clinics are typically several weeks for non-urgent conditions.</p><p>For common skin conditions — eczema, acne, rashes, and infections — GP-level assessment covers the majority of clinical need. Our service provides this assessment same day, from anywhere in Romania, in English, Romanian. When specialist dermatology input is genuinely needed, your doctor issues a formal referral letter on the same day.</p>

heroTitle

Before
Weight Management — Medical Assessment and Personalised Plan
After
Medical weight management review

detailBody

Before
<p>Struggling to lose weight despite making changes to your diet and lifestyle? For most people who cannot shift weight, there are underlying medical, hormonal, and metabolic factors at play that no generic diet plan addresses — because they require clinical assessment, not calorie counting.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer comprehensive weight management consultations by secure video call — identifying the clinical factors contributing to your weight, building a personalised management plan, and providing the medical supervision that makes sustainable change possible.</p><p>This is not a diet programme. It is medical care, provided by doctors, for a medical condition.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adults who:</p><ul><li>Have difficulty losing weight despite dietary and lifestyle changes</li><li>Have a BMI of 25 or above and are seeking structured medical support</li></ul><p>Have weight-related conditions — including pre-diabetes, insulin resistance, hypertension, or PCOS — that are contributing to or complicating their weight</p><p>Want to understand whether they may be candidates for medical weight management therapies following clinical assessment</p><ul><li>Have tried diet and exercise approaches without sustainable results</li><li>Are preparing for or considering bariatric surgery and are seeking medical assessment</li><li>Want ongoing medical monitoring of their metabolic health alongside weight management</li><li>Are expats or international residents in Romania who want weight management support in English</li></ul><h2>Why Medical Supervision Makes a Difference</h2><p>Most weight management approaches fail not through lack of effort but because they do not address the underlying clinical picture. Hormonal imbalance, insulin resistance, medication side effects, sleep disorders, and metabolic dysfunction all contribute to weight gain in ways that diet and exercise alone cannot resolve.</p><p>Specifically:</p><ul><li>Insulin resistance causes the body to store fat regardless of caloric deficit</li><li>Hypothyroidism slows metabolism — often significantly — independent of diet</li><li>PCOS disrupts hormonal balance and makes weight loss genuinely more difficult</li><li>Cortisol dysregulation affects how the body distributes fat</li></ul><p>Medication side effects — antidepressants, antipsychotics, corticosteroids, beta-blockers — can cause significant weight gain as a direct pharmacological effect</p><p>A diet that ignores these factors will fail — not because of insufficient effort, but because it is solving the wrong problem. Medical assessment identifies the specific causes and proposes a plan that works with your biology.</p><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Comprehensive medical assessmentYour doctor reviews your medical history, current medications, metabolic risk factors, hormonal profile, dietary patterns, physical activity, sleep quality, stress levels, and previous weight management attempts — building a complete clinical picture of the factors contributing to your weight.</p><p>Identification of underlying causesYour doctor assesses hormonal contributors — including thyroid dysfunction, insulin resistance, PCOS, and cortisol dysregulation — metabolic factors, medication-related weight gain, and lifestyle elements that interact with your biology.</p><p>Personalised weight management planBased on the assessment, your doctor develops a structured, evidence-based plan adapted to your BMI, health status, lifestyle, and long-term goals — including nutritional strategy, behavioural approach, metabolic health optimisation, and clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion.</p><p>Assessment for medical weight management therapiesFor patients who meet the clinical criteria, your doctor assesses eligibility for evidence-based medical weight management therapies as part of a comprehensive clinical assessment. Eligibility is determined by clinical criteria — BMI, weight-related comorbidities, previous treatment history, contraindications, and general health status — not by patient preference.</p><p>Investigation requests where indicatedWhere blood tests are clinically indicated — thyroid function, fasting glucose, HbA1c, hormonal profile, lipid panel — your doctor issues the request on the same day.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through SIPE. You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Ongoing reviewWeight management is a long-term clinical process. Follow-up consultations are available to monitor progress, review treatment response, and adjust your plan.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Metabolic</li><li>Overweight and obesity — BMI 25 and above</li><li>Insulin resistance and pre-diabetes</li><li>Type 2 diabetes — weight management as part of metabolic control</li><li>Metabolic syndrome</li><li>High cholesterol and triglycerides</li><li>Hormonal</li><li>Polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS)</li><li>Hypothyroidism associated with weight gain</li><li>Cortisol dysregulation and stress-related weight gain</li><li>Metabolic changes associated with menopause</li><li>Cardiovascular</li><li>Hypertension associated with excess weight</li><li>Cardiovascular risk reduction through weight management</li><li>Other</li><li>Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease</li><li>Obstructive sleep apnoea</li><li>Joint pain related to excess weight</li><li>Fertility concerns related to weight and hormonal status</li></ul><h2>Weight Management in Romania — What to Expect</h2><p>Private weight management services in Romania are available but predominantly clinic-based and concentrated in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. For patients outside Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara, or for anyone without a registered family doctor, accessing structured medical weight management is practically difficult — regardless of language preference.</p><p>Global Health provides comprehensive medical weight management consultations in English — including full metabolic assessment, personalised management planning, and assessment for medical weight management therapies where clinically appropriate — by secure video call, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Is this a diet programme or a medical service?It is a medical service. Our doctors conduct a full clinical assessment of the medical, hormonal, and metabolic factors contributing to your weight and develop a personalised management plan based on clinical findings. It is not a diet plan, a meal replacement programme, or a fitness coaching service.</p><p>Can I access medical weight management therapies through this service?Newer metabolic therapies for weight management may be appropriate following full clinical assessment. Eligibility is determined by clinical criteria — including BMI, weight-related conditions, contraindications, and general health status — and the decision is made solely by the doctor following complete assessment. No specific therapy can be confirmed or prescribed in advance of a full clinical review.</p><p>What blood tests might be needed?Depending on your clinical presentation, your doctor may request thyroid function, fasting glucose, HbA1c, hormonal panel, and lipid profile as part of the assessment. Investigation requests can be coordinated in the same consultation.</p><p>How many consultations will I need?This depends on your individual clinical picture. An initial consultation establishes your baseline and management plan. Follow-up consultations monitor progress and adjust the plan. Your doctor advises on the appropriate review schedule.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance?No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions issued through this service are dispensed at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p><p>Is this service suitable for adolescents?Weight management consultations are primarily for adults. For adolescent weight concerns, your doctor advises on appropriate referral.</p>
After

serviceTranslations / cmr9d1o0a0009vcjue0do2bpf EN

Update only the following fields; preserve the rest of this row.

seoTitle

Before
Musculoskeletal & Pain Assessment Online | Romania | Same Day
After
Muscle and joint pain assessment | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Assessment for back pain, joint pain, sciatica, and muscle injuries. Our CMR-registered doctors assess musculoskeletal conditions by secure video call. Same-day appointments available anywhere in Romania.
After
Muscle and joint pain assessment. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Musculoskeletal & Pain Assessment — Online, Same Day
After
Muscle and joint pain assessment

detailBody

Before
<p>Back pain, joint pain, sciatica, muscle injuries, and chronic musculoskeletal conditions are among the most common reasons people seek medical care — and among the most poorly served by the current primary care model. Waiting weeks for an orthopaedic or rheumatology appointment for a condition that needs assessment today is a structural problem across both public and private healthcare in Romania.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), conduct comprehensive musculoskeletal assessments by secure video call — identifying the underlying cause, assessing functional impact, advising on evidence-based management, and coordinating referral and investigation where clinically indicated. Same day, from anywhere in Romania, in English, Romanian.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adults with:</p><ul><li>An acute musculoskeletal injury or sudden-onset pain requiring clinical assessment</li><li>Chronic or ongoing pain that has not been adequately assessed or managed</li><li>A known musculoskeletal condition that is flaring and requires timely review</li><li>Pain following an injury, procedure, or period of immobilisation</li></ul><p>Musculoskeletal symptoms associated with a chronic condition — diabetes, cardiovascular disease, or inflammatory conditions — that complicate management</p><p>A need for physiotherapy referral, imaging requests, or specialist referral coordinated through a clinical assessment</p><p>Anyone who has been told "it is normal" or "just rest" without receiving a structured clinical explanation of what is happening and what to do</p><p>If you are experiencing sudden, intense pain, chest pain, pain following significant trauma, or any symptom that seriously concerns you — go to the nearest emergency department or call 112 immediately.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Spine and neurological</li><li>Acute and chronic low back pain</li><li>Neck pain and cervical spine disorders</li><li>Sciatica and lumbar radiculopathy</li><li>Disc herniation and associated nerve compression symptoms</li><li>Thoracic spine pain</li><li>Joint conditions</li><li>Knee pain — including osteoarthritis, ligament injuries, and patellofemoral syndrome</li><li>Hip pain — including osteoarthritis, bursitis, and referred pain</li><li>Shoulder pain — including rotator cuff injury, subacromial impingement, and adhesive capsulitis</li><li>Wrist and hand pain — including carpal tunnel syndrome and tendinopathy</li><li>Ankle and foot pain — including plantar fasciitis and Achilles tendinopathy</li><li>Elbow pain — including lateral and medial epicondylitis</li><li>Muscle and soft tissue</li><li>Muscle strains and tears</li><li>Soft tissue injuries</li><li>Tendinitis and tendinopathy</li><li>Bursitis</li><li>Sports injuries and activity-related musculoskeletal conditions</li><li>Chronic and systemic conditions</li><li>Osteoarthritis — assessment, management, and referral coordination</li><li>Inflammatory arthritis — assessment and rheumatology referral where indicated</li><li>Fibromyalgia — assessment and integrated management</li><li>Neuropathic pain — assessment and management pathway</li><li>Chronic pain syndromes — structured assessment and care coordination</li><li>Post-injury or post-surgical pain — review and management plan</li><li>Work and lifestyle related</li><li>Work-related musculoskeletal conditions — repetitive strain injuries, postural pain</li><li>Ergonomic assessment and advice</li><li>Sports and exercise-related injury assessment</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessmentYour doctor takes a detailed history of your pain — onset, location, character, intensity, radiation, aggravating and relieving factors, functional impact, and systematic screening for red flags. Any existing imaging results, investigation reports, or clinical letters are reviewed in full.</p><p>Red flag screeningMusculoskeletal assessment always includes systematic screening for red flags — indicators that pain may have a serious underlying cause requiring urgent investigation or referral. Your doctor assesses these as a standard part of every consultation.</p><p>Personalised management planBased on the assessment, your doctor advises on the most appropriate approach — which may include structured physiotherapy and rehabilitation referral, imaging requests where clinically indicated, specialist referral where needed, ergonomic and lifestyle advice, and clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><p>Referral and investigation coordinationWhen physiotherapy referral, imaging, or specialist input is clinically indicated, your doctor issues the relevant documentation on the same day — so you leave the consultation with a clear clinical pathway, not just a recommendation to figure it out yourself.</p><h2>Red Flags — When to Seek Emergency Care</h2><p>Musculoskeletal pain is almost always benign — but some presentations require urgent assessment that cannot wait for a video consultation.</p><p>Seek emergency care immediately if your pain is accompanied by:</p><p>Any change in bladder or bowel function associated with back pain — incontinence or retention — this may indicate cauda equina syndrome, a surgical emergency</p><ul><li>Significant trauma — fall from height, road traffic accident, or significant impact</li><li>Sudden severe chest pain — which may indicate a cardiac or aortic cause rather than musculoskeletal</li><li>Unexplained weight loss associated with new or worsening pain</li><li>Fever associated with swelling and joint pain — may indicate septic arthritis</li><li>Rapidly progressive neurological symptoms — weakness, numbness, or loss of coordination</li><li>Constant, severe pain that does not respond to any position or movement</li></ul><p>If in doubt, always err on the side of caution and seek emergency care.</p><h2>Why Early Clinical Assessment Makes a Difference</h2><p>Most musculoskeletal conditions are self-limiting — they resolve with adequate rest, activity modification, and structured rehabilitation. But without clinical assessment, patients frequently either under-treat — returning to activity too early and causing further injury — or over-treat — restricting activity unnecessarily and prolonging recovery.</p><p>Early clinical assessment identifies what is actually happening, excludes serious underlying pathology, and provides a structured, evidence-based management plan that allows full functional recovery more quickly and safely than waiting for the situation to resolve itself.</p><p>For chronic conditions — osteoarthritis, inflammatory arthritis, fibromyalgia, chronic pain syndromes — early clinical involvement with a structured management plan significantly improves long-term functional outcomes and quality of life.</p><h2>Musculoskeletal Care in Romania — Access and Waiting Times</h2><p>Orthopaedic and rheumatology waiting times in Romania are long — particularly in the public system, where waiting times for non-urgent specialist appointments can extend to several months. Private orthopaedic care is available in major cities but concentrated in Bucharest and a small number of regional centres.</p><p>For the majority of musculoskeletal presentations — acute injuries, back pain, joint pain, tendinopathies, and chronic pain management — GP-level assessment and management covers the primary clinical need without requiring immediate specialist orthopaedic or rheumatology input. Our service provides this assessment same day, from anywhere in Romania, with referral and imaging coordination where specialist input is genuinely needed.</p><p>Physiotherapy in Romania is widely available privately — but most physiotherapists require a referral letter from a doctor. Our doctors issue physiotherapy referral letters on the same day as the consultation, addressed to the physiotherapist or clinic of your choice.</p><h2>What Is Cauda Equina Syndrome</h2><p>Cauda equina syndrome is a rare but serious spinal emergency caused by compression of the nerve roots at the base of the spine. It presents as back pain associated with change in bladder or bowel function — including incontinence or difficulty urinating — and requires emergency surgical assessment.</p><p>It is mentioned prominently on this page because it can initially be confused with common back pain. If you have back pain associated with any change in bladder or bowel function, go to the nearest emergency department immediately — do not wait for a video consultation, do not wait to see if it resolves.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Can a musculoskeletal condition really be assessed by video call?Yes — for the vast majority of presentations. The clinical history is the most powerful diagnostic tool in musculoskeletal medicine and a full history can be taken by video call. Your doctor also observes your movement, posture, and functional capacity during the call. Where physical examination findings that cannot be assessed remotely are needed, your doctor advises on appropriate in-person assessment.</p><p>What if I need an MRI or X-ray?If imaging is clinically indicated based on the assessment, your doctor issues a formal imaging request on the same day. You can then book the imaging at a private radiology centre of your choice in Romania — private radiology is widely accessible in major cities and in many regional centres.</p><p>Can I get a physiotherapy referral through this service?Yes. Physiotherapy referral letters are issued when clinically indicated — addressed to the physiotherapist or clinic of your choice. Your doctor can advise on what to look for in a physiotherapist for your specific condition.</p><p>What is cauda equina syndrome and why is it mentioned?Cauda equina syndrome is a rare but serious spinal emergency caused by compression of nerve roots at the base of the spine. It presents as back pain associated with change in bladder or bowel function. If you have back pain with any change in bladder or bowel function, go to the nearest emergency department immediately — do not wait for a video consultation.</p><p>Is this service suitable for sports injuries?Yes. Sports and exercise-related musculoskeletal injuries are a core part of this service — including acute injuries, overuse conditions, and return-to-sport assessment following injury.</p><p>I already have imaging results — can the doctor review them?Yes. Bring any existing imaging results, reports, or clinical letters to your consultation. Your doctor reviews them in full and incorporates them into the management plan.</p><p>Do I need to have had imaging done before booking?No. Your doctor assesses whether imaging is clinically indicated and requests it where appropriate. If you already have imaging, bring the results — your doctor reviews them in full.</p><p>Can I use this service without a registered family doctor?Yes. This service is available to anyone in Romania — no prior registration, no family doctor required, no health insurance needed.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance?No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions and referrals issued through this service are dispensed and accepted on a private basis regardless of insurance status.</p>
After

serviceTranslations / cmrfhr6rw008mywju1t748alu CS

seoTitle

Before
Lékař online bez registrace | Konzultace tentýž den | Global Health
After
Online lékařská konzultace | Rumunsko

seoDescription

Before
Lékař registrovaný u CMR dostupný online, bez čekací listiny a bez registrace u praktického lékaře. Videokonzultace v rumunštině nebo angličtině. Rezervujte si dnes.
After
Online lékařská konzultace. Konzultace v rumunštině a angličtině. Před rezervací si ověřte lékaře a dostupné termíny.

heroTitle

Before
Online lékař — bez registrace, bez čekárny, ještě tentýž den
After
Online lékařská konzultace

detailBody

Before
<p>Potřebujete dnes lékaře, ale nejste zapsáni na seznamu praktického lékaře? Nebo bydlíte v oblasti, kde je přístup k praktickému lékaři na vhodném místě obtížný? Naši lékaři, registrovaní u Colegiul Medicilor din România (CMR), poskytují kompletní klinické konzultace prostřednictvím zabezpečené videokonference — bez nutnosti být předem registrovaným pacientem, bez čekárny, s termínem ještě tentýž den.</p><h2>Realita zdravotnického systému v Rumunsku</h2><p>Přístup k primární zdravotní péči v Rumunsku je nerovnoměrný. Ve velkých městech — București, Cluj-Napoca, Timișoara, Iași — existují relativně dostupné soukromé možnosti. Ale mimo ně, nebo u osob, které nejsou zapsány na seznamu praktického lékaře, může být získání lékařské konzultace včas obtížné.</p><p>Global Health existuje právě proto, aby tuto mezeru zaplnil. Naši lékaři, registrovaní u CMR, poskytují lékařské konzultace prostřednictvím videokonference — v rumunštině, angličtině— ještě tentýž den, odkudkoli z Rumunska, bez předchozí registrace.</p><p>Máte již praktického lékaře? Naše služba funguje doplňkově — pro akutní potíže, druhý lékařský názor nebo situace, kdy potřebujete mluvit s lékařem dnes, ne při další dostupné návštěvě.</p><h2>Co léčíme</h2><p>Naši lékaři posuzují a řeší širokou škálu akutních a neurgentních obtíží:</p><ul><li>Respirační a ORL</li><li>Nachlazení, chřipka, sinusitida a bronchitida</li><li>Příznaky COVID-19 a post-COVID obtíže</li><li>Bolest v krku, angína a ušní infekce</li><li>Plicní infekce a přetrvávající kašel</li><li>Mírné astmatické záchvaty</li><li>Infekce</li><li>Infekce močových cest — vyšetření a předpis antibiotika, kde je to klinicky indikováno</li><li>Kožní infekce</li><li>Respirační infekce vyžadující antibiotickou léčbu</li><li>Gastrointestinální infekce</li><li>Obecné příznaky</li><li>Horečka u dospělých</li><li>Bolesti hlavy a migréna</li><li>Nevolnost, zvracení, průjem a bolesti břicha</li><li>Únava a celková schvácenost</li><li>Kůže a oči</li><li>Vyrážky, ekzémy a mírné alergické reakce</li><li>Zánět spojivek a mírné podráždění očí</li><li>Pohybový aparát</li><li>Bolesti zad, šíje a svalů</li><li>Drobné úrazy pohybového aparátu</li><li>Ostatní</li><li>Obavy týkající se stávajících léků nebo probíhající léčby</li><li>Medicínské dokumenty — lékařská potvrzení, doporučující dopisy</li><li>Rady pro cestovní medicínu</li><li>Druhý lékařský názor</li></ul><h2>Co konzultace zahrnuje</h2><p>Kompletní klinické zhodnoceníLékař provede úplnou anamnézu vašich příznaků, relevantní zdravotní historii a aktuální léky — a provede strukturované klinické zhodnocení prostřednictvím videokonference, ve stejném standardu jako osobní konzultace.</p><p>Jasné vysvětleníNáš lékař si vyhradí čas, aby vám vysvětlil, co se děje, co máte dělat dál a kdy vyhledat další péči. Zapojení pacienta do lékařského rozhodování není luxus — je to náš standard péče.</p><p>Elektronický předpis — kde je klinicky indikovánoKdyž je to klinicky indikováno, lékař vystaví elektronický předpis ověřený v Sistemul Informatic de Prescripție Electronică (SIPE). Předložíte jej v jakékoli lékárně v Rumunsku.</p><p>Předpis antibiotika — kde je klinicky indikovánoOd roku 2024 vyžadují všechna antibiotika v Rumunsku lékařský předpis. Lékař vyhodnotí vaše příznaky a předepíše vhodné antibiotikum tam, kde je to klinicky odůvodněno — včetně infekcí močových cest, respiračních infekcí a kožních infekcí — při dodržení zásad antibiotického stewardshipu.</p><p>Doporučující dopisy a zdravotnická dokumentaceKdyž jsou potřeba doporučení ke specialistovi, krevní testy, zobrazovací vyšetření nebo jiná klinická dokumentace, lékař je vystaví ještě tentýž den.</p><p>Pracovní neschopnostPokud jste zaměstnáni v Rumunsku a pracovní neschopnost je klinicky indikována, lékař může vystavit příslušnou dokumentaci. Rozhodnutí o vystavení pracovní neschopnosti je vždy klinické rozhodnutí lékaře po úplném vyšetření.</p><h2>Proč Global Health</h2><p>Bez předchozí registraceNemusíte být registrovaným pacientem. Objednejte se, konzultujte, získejte jasný klinický výsledek — bez byrokracie, bez čekání na přijetí na seznam.</p><p>Lékaři registrovaní u CMRVšechny konzultace jsou prováděny lékaři registrovanými u Colegiul Medicilor din România, v souladu s rumunskými standardy telemedicíny podle zákona č. 95/2006 a HG č. 1133/2022.</p><p>Objednání tentýž denDostupnost tentýž den, sedm dní v týdnu, včetně večerů a víkendů.</p><p>Dostupné z celého RumunskaNemusíte být v București. Můžete být kdekoli v Rumunsku — potřebujete pouze stabilní internetové připojení a zařízení s kamerou.</p><p>Tři jazykyRumunština, angličtina — podle dostupnosti lékaře.</p><p>Elektronický předpis a pracovní neschopnostNaši lékaři mohou vystavit elektronické předpisy prostřednictvím SIPE a potvrzení o pracovní neschopnosti — stejně jako jakýkoli praktický lékař registrovaný v Rumunsku.</p><h2>Často kladené otázky</h2><p>Musím mít rumunské zdravotní pojištění, abych mohl/a využít tuto službu?Ne. Jedná se o soukromou službu, hrazenou přímo za konzultaci — není vyžadováno veřejné rumunské zdravotní pojištění, žádná registrace, žádné členství. Některá soukromá pojištění pokrývají telemedicínské konzultace — ověřte si to u své pojišťovny.</p><p>Může lékař předepsat antibiotika?Ano — kde je to klinicky indikováno po úplném zhodnocení vašich příznaků. Od roku 2024 vyžadují všechna antibiotika v Rumunsku lékařský předpis. Lékař posoudí váš stav a předepíše vhodné antibiotikum tam, kde klinické důkazy toto podporují, při dodržení zásad antibiotického stewardshipu.</p><p>Může lékař vystavit elektronický předpis?Ano. Naši lékaři jsou registrováni u CMR a v Sistemul Informatic de Prescripție Electronică (SIPE). Elektronické předpisy jsou vystavovány přímo — předložíte je v jakékoli lékárně v Rumunsku.</p><p>Musím být v București, abych mohl/a tuto službu využít?Ne. Služba je dostupná z jakéhokoli místa v Rumunsku — potřebujete pouze stabilní internetové připojení a zařízení s kamerou.</p><p>Jak rychle mohu získat termín?Termíny ještě tentýž den jsou k dispozici. Rezervujte online a termín je potvrzen okamžitě.</p><p>Jaké jazyky jsou dostupné?Rumunština, angličtina— podle dostupnosti lékaře.</p><p>Mohu touto službou získat pracovní neschopnost?Ano. Pokud jste zaměstnáni v Rumunsku a pracovní neschopnost je klinicky indikována, lékař může vystavit příslušnou dokumentaci. Rozhodnutí je vždy klinické, učiněné po úplném vyšetření.</p><p>Je služba vhodná pro děti?U akutních pediatrických onemocnění může služba nabídnout počáteční zhodnocení a doporučení. U složitých nebo přetrvávajících pediatrických stavů lékař doporučí odeslání k osobnímu vyšetření.</p><p>Co se stane, pokud můj stav vyžaduje osobní péči?Pokud lékař určí, že váš stav vyžaduje osobní vyšetření nebo urgentní péči, budete okamžitě informováni a směrováni do příslušné služby. Vaše bezpečnost je vždy klinickou prioritou.</p>

Ale mimo ně, nebo u osob, které nejsou zapsány na seznamu praktického lékaře, může být získání lékařské konzultace včas obtížné.</p><p>Global Health existuje právě proto, aby tuto mezeru zaplnil. Naši lékaři, registrovaní u CMR, poskytují lékařské konzultace prostřednictvím videokonference — v rumunštině, angličtině— ještě tentýž den, odkudkoli z Rumunska, bez předchozí registrace.</p><p>Máte již praktického lékaře? Naše služba funguje doplňkově — pro akutní potíže, druhý lékařský názor nebo situace, kdy potřebujete mluvit s lékařem dnes, ne při další dostupné návštěvě.</p><h2>Co léčíme</h2><p>Naši lékaři posuzují a řeší širokou škálu akutních a neurgentních obtíží:</p><ul><li>Respirační a ORL</li><li>Nachlazení, chřipka, sinusitida a bronchitida</li><li>Příznaky COVID-19 a post-COVID obtíže</li><li>Bolest v krku, angína a ušní infekce</li><li>Plicní infekce a přetrvávající kašel</li><li>Mírné astmatické záchvaty</li><li>Infekce</li><li>Infekce močových cest — vyšetření a předpis antibiotika, kde je to klinicky indikováno</li><li>Kožní infekce</li><li>Respirační infekce vyžadující antibiotickou léčbu</li><li>Gastrointestinální infekce</li><li>Obecné příznaky</li><li>Horečka u dospělých</li><li>Bolesti hlavy a migréna</li><li>Nevolnost, zvracení, průjem a bolesti břicha</li><li>Únava a celková schvácenost</li><li>Kůže a oči</li><li>Vyrážky, ekzémy a mírné alergické reakce</li><li>Zánět spojivek a mírné podráždění očí</li><li>Pohybový aparát</li><li>Bolesti zad, šíje a svalů</li><li>Drobné úrazy pohybového aparátu</li><li>Ostatní</li><li>Obavy týkající se stávajících léků nebo probíhající léčby</li><li>Medicínské dokumenty — lékařská potvrzení, doporučující dopisy</li><li>Rady pro cestovní medicínu</li><li>Druhý lékařský názor</li></ul><h2>Co konzultace zahrnuje</h2><p>Kompletní klinické zhodnoceníLékař provede úplnou anamnézu vašich příznaků, relevantní zdravotní historii a aktuální léky — a provede strukturované klinické zhodnocení prostřednictvím videokonference, ve stejném standardu jako osobní konzultace.</p><p>Jasné vysvětleníNáš lékař si vyhradí čas, aby vám vysvětlil, co se děje, co máte dělat dál a kdy vyhledat další péči. Zapojení pacienta do lékařského rozhodování není luxus — je to náš standard péče.</p><p>Elektronický předpis — kde je klinicky indikovánoKdyž je to klinicky indikováno, lékař vystaví elektronický předpis ověřený v Sistemul Informatic de Prescripție Electronică (SIPE). Předložíte jej v jakékoli lékárně v Rumunsku.</p><p>Předpis antibiotika — kde je klinicky indikovánoOd roku 2024 vyžadují všechna antibiotika v Rumunsku lékařský předpis. Lékař vyhodnotí vaše příznaky a předepíše vhodné antibiotikum tam, kde je to klinicky odůvodněno — včetně infekcí močových cest, respiračních infekcí a kožních infekcí — při dodržení zásad antibiotického stewardshipu.</p><p>Doporučující dopisy a zdravotnická dokumentaceKdyž jsou potřeba doporučení ke specialistovi, krevní testy, zobrazovací vyšetření nebo jiná klinická dokumentace, lékař je vystaví ještě tentýž den.</p><p>Pracovní neschopnostPokud jste zaměstnáni v Rumunsku a pracovní neschopnost je klinicky indikována, lékař může vystavit příslušnou dokumentaci. Rozhodnutí o vystavení pracovní neschopnosti je vždy klinické rozhodnutí lékaře po úplném vyšetření.</p><h2>Proč Global Health</h2><p>Bez předchozí registraceNemusíte být registrovaným pacientem. Objednejte se, konzultujte, získejte jasný klinický výsledek — bez byrokracie, bez čekání na přijetí na seznam.</p><p>Lékaři registrovaní u CMRVšechny konzultace jsou prováděny lékaři registrovanými u Colegiul Medicilor din România, v souladu s rumunskými standardy telemedicíny podle zákona č. 95/2006 a HG č. 1133/2022.</p><p>Objednání tentýž denDostupnost tentýž den, sedm dní v týdnu, včetně večerů a víkendů.</p><p>Dostupné z celého RumunskaNemusíte být v București. Můžete být kdekoli v Rumunsku — potřebujete pouze stabilní internetové připojení a zařízení s kamerou.</p><p>Tři jazykyRumunština, angličtina — podle dostupnosti lékaře.</p><p>Elektronický předpis a pracovní neschopnostNaši lékaři mohou vystavit elektronické předpisy prostřednictvím SIPE a potvrzení o pracovní neschopnosti — stejně jako jakýkoli praktický lékař registrovaný v Rumunsku.</p>

heroTitle

Before
Online-Arzt — ohne Registrierung, ohne Wartezimmer, noch am selben Tag
After
Ärztliche Onlineberatung

detailBody

Before
<p>Brauchen Sie heute einen Arzt, sind aber nicht auf der Liste eines Hausarztes eingetragen? Oder leben Sie in einer Gegend, in der der Zugang zu einem geeigneten Hausarzt schwierig ist? Unsere beim Rumänischen Ärztekolleg (CMR) registrierten Ärzte führen vollständige klinische Konsultationen per gesicherter Videokonferenz durch — ohne dass Sie zuvor als Patient registriert sein müssen, ohne Wartezimmer, mit Termin am selben Tag.</p><h2>Eine Realität des Gesundheitssystems in Rumänien</h2><p>Der Zugang zur primären medizinischen Versorgung in Rumänien ist uneinheitlich. In den großen Städten — Bukarest, Cluj-Napoca, Timișoara, Iași — gibt es relativ erschwingliche private Optionen. Aber außerhalb davon oder für Personen, die nicht auf der Liste eines Hausarztes eingetragen sind, kann es schwierig sein, rechtzeitig eine ärztliche Konsultation zu erhalten.</p><p>Global Health existiert genau, um diese Lücke zu schließen. Unsere beim CMR registrierten Ärzte bieten medizinische Konsultationen per Videokonferenz an — auf Rumänisch, Englisch — am selben Tag, von jedem Ort in Rumänien aus, ohne vorherige Registrierung.</p><p>Haben Sie bereits einen Hausarzt? Unser Dienst funktioniert ergänzend — für akute Probleme, eine zweite ärztliche Meinung oder Situationen, in denen Sie heute mit einem Arzt sprechen müssen und nicht erst beim nächsten verfügbaren Termin.</p><h2>Was wir behandeln</h2><p>Unsere Ärzte beurteilen und behandeln eine breite Palette akuter und nicht dringender Erkrankungen:</p><ul><li>Atemwege und HNO</li><li>Erkältung, Grippe, Sinusitis und Bronchitis</li><li>COVID-19-Symptome und Post-COVID-Probleme</li><li>Halsschmerzen, Mandelentzündung und Ohrinfektionen</li><li>Lungeninfektionen und anhaltender Husten</li><li>Leichte Asthmaanfälle</li><li>Infektionen</li><li>Harnwegsinfektionen — Beurteilung und Antibiotikaverschreibung, wenn klinisch indiziert</li><li>Hautinfektionen</li><li>Atemwegsinfektionen, die eine antibiotische Beurteilung erfordern</li><li>Magen-Darm-Infektionen</li><li>Allgemeine Symptome</li><li>Fieber bei Erwachsenen</li><li>Kopfschmerzen und Migräne</li><li>Übelkeit, Erbrechen, Durchfall und Bauchschmerzen</li><li>Müdigkeit und allgemeines Krankheitsgefühl</li><li>Haut und Augen</li><li>Hautausschläge, Ekzeme und leichte allergische Reaktionen</li><li>Bindehautentzündung und leichte Augenreizungen</li><li>Bewegungsapparat</li><li>Rücken-, Nacken- und Muskelschmerzen</li><li>Leichte Verletzungen des Bewegungsapparats</li><li>Sonstiges</li><li>Bedenken hinsichtlich aktueller Medikamente oder laufender Behandlung</li><li>Medizinische Dokumente — ärztliche Bescheinigungen, Überweisungsschreiben</li><li>Reisemedizinische Beratung</li><li>Zweite ärztliche Meinung</li></ul><h2>Was die Konsultation umfasst</h2><p>Vollständige klinische BeurteilungDer Arzt erhebt eine vollständige Anamnese Ihrer Symptome, Ihrer relevanten Krankengeschichte und Ihrer aktuellen Medikamente — und führt per Videokonferenz eine strukturierte klinische Beurteilung durch, auf demselben Standard wie eine persönliche Konsultation.</p><p>Klare ErklärungUnser Arzt nimmt sich Zeit, Ihnen zu erklären, was vor sich geht, was Sie als Nächstes tun sollten und wann Sie weitere medizinische Hilfe in Anspruch nehmen sollten. Die Einbeziehung des Patienten in die medizinische Entscheidung ist kein Luxus — sie ist unser Versorgungsstandard.</p><p>Elektronische Verschreibung — wo klinisch indiziertWenn klinisch angezeigt, stellt der Arzt ein elektronisches Rezept aus, das im System Informatic de Prescripție Electronică (SIPE) validiert wird. Sie legen es in jeder Apotheke in Rumänien vor.</p><p>Antibiotikaverschreibung — wo klinisch indiziertSeit 2024 sind alle Antibiotika in Rumänien verschreibungspflichtig. Der Arzt beurteilt Ihre Symptome und verschreibt das geeignete Antibiotikum, wo dies klinisch gerechtfertigt ist — einschließlich bei Harnwegsinfektionen, Atemwegsinfektionen und Hautinfektionen — unter Beachtung der Grundsätze des Antibiotic Stewardship.</p><p>Überweisungsschreiben und medizinische DokumentationWenn Überweisungen zu Fachärzten, Blutuntersuchungen, bildgebende Verfahren oder andere klinische Dokumentation erforderlich sind, stellt der Arzt diese noch am selben Tag aus.</p><p>KrankschreibungWenn Sie in Rumänien beschäftigt sind und eine Krankschreibung klinisch indiziert ist, kann der Arzt die entsprechenden Unterlagen ausstellen. Die Entscheidung über die Ausstellung einer Krankschreibung ist immer eine klinische Entscheidung des Arztes nach vollständiger Beurteilung.</p><h2>Warum Global Health</h2><p>Keine vorherige RegistrierungSie müssen kein registrierter Patient sein. Buchen Sie, lassen Sie sich untersuchen, erhalten Sie ein klares klinisches Ergebnis — ohne Bürokratie, ohne auf die Aufnahme auf eine Liste warten zu müssen.</p><p>Bei CMR registrierte ÄrzteAlle Konsultationen werden von Ärzten durchgeführt, die beim Colegiul Medicilor din România registriert sind, gemäß den rumänischen Telemedizinstandards nach dem Gesetz Nr. 95/2006 und der Regierungsentscheidung Nr. 1133/2022.</p><p>Termin am selben TagVerfügbarkeit am selben Tag, sieben Tage pro Woche, einschließlich Abende und Wochenenden.</p><p>Von überall in Rumänien zugänglichSie müssen nicht in Bukarest sein. Sie können überall in Rumänien wohnen — Sie benötigen nur eine stabile Internetverbindung und ein Gerät mit Kamera.</p><p>Drei SprachenRumänisch, Englisch — je nach Verfügbarkeit des Arztes.</p><p>Elektronisches Rezept und KrankschreibungUnsere Ärzte können elektronische Rezepte über SIPE und Krankschreibungen ausstellen — genau wie jeder in Rumänien registrierte Hausarzt.</p><h2>Häufig gestellte Fragen</h2><p>Muss ich eine rumänische Krankenversicherung haben, um diesen Dienst zu nutzen?Nein. Dies ist ein privater Dienst, der direkt pro Konsultation bezahlt wird — keine rumänische gesetzliche Krankenversicherung, keine Registrierung, keine Mitgliedschaft erforderlich. Einige private Versicherungen decken telemedizinische Konsultationen ab — erkundigen Sie sich bei Ihrem Versicherer.</p><p>Kann der Arzt Antibiotika verschreiben?Ja — wo dies nach einer vollständigen Beurteilung Ihrer Symptome klinisch indiziert ist. Seit 2024 sind alle Antibiotika in Rumänien verschreibungspflichtig. Der Arzt beurteilt Ihre Vorstellung und verschreibt das geeignete Antibiotikum, wo die klinische Evidenz dies unterstützt, unter Beachtung der Grundsätze des Antibiotic Stewardship.</p><p>Kann der Arzt ein elektronisches Rezept ausstellen?Ja. Unsere Ärzte sind beim CMR und im System Informatic de Prescripție Electronică (SIPE) registriert. Elektronische Rezepte werden direkt ausgestellt — Sie legen sie in jeder Apotheke in Rumänien vor.</p><p>Muss ich in Bukarest sein, um diesen Dienst zu nutzen?Nein. Der Dienst ist von jedem Ort in Rumänien aus verfügbar — Sie benötigen nur eine stabile Internetverbindung und ein Gerät mit Kamera.</p><p>Wie schnell kann ich einen Termin erhalten?Termine am selben Tag sind verfügbar. Buchen Sie online und der Termin wird sofort bestätigt.</p><p>Welche Sprachen sind verfügbar?Rumänisch, Englisch — je nach Verfügbarkeit des Arztes.</p><p>Kann ich über diesen Dienst eine Krankschreibung erhalten?Ja. Wenn Sie in Rumänien beschäftigt sind und eine Krankschreibung klinisch indiziert ist, kann der Arzt die entsprechenden Unterlagen ausstellen. Die Entscheidung ist immer klinisch und wird nach vollständiger Beurteilung getroffen.</p><p>Ist der Dienst für Kinder geeignet?Bei akuten pädiatrischen Erkrankungen kann der Dienst eine erste Beurteilung und Orientierung bieten. Bei komplexen oder anhaltenden pädiatrischen Vorstellungen wird der Arzt eine Überweisung zur persönlichen Untersuchung empfehlen.</p><p>Was passiert, wenn mein Zustand eine persönliche Behandlung erfordert?Wenn der Arzt feststellt, dass Ihr Zustand eine persönliche Untersuchung oder eine Notfallbehandlung erfordert, werden Sie umgehend informiert und an die entsprechende Versorgung weitergeleitet. Ihre Sicherheit hat immer oberste klinische Priorität.</p>

Online medical consultation | Romania

seoDescription

Before
See an English-speaking doctor registered to practise in Romania today. No registration required, no waiting room. Video consultation from anywhere in Romania. Book now.
After
Online medical consultation. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
See an English-Speaking Doctor Today — Online, From Anywhere in Romania
After
Online medical consultation

detailBody

Before
<p>Need to see a doctor today — in English, without a Romanian GP registration? Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer full clinical assessments by secure video call. No waiting rooms, no language barrier, no need to already be registered with a Romanian family doctor.</p><p>Whether you are an expat, international student, business traveller, digital nomad, or anyone in Romania who needs to speak to a doctor today — we are here, same day, from anywhere in the country.</p><h2>Why Expats Choose This Service</h2><p>Finding English-speaking primary care in Romania is genuinely difficult outside a handful of private clinics concentrated in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. Most require prior registration, operate only during weekday clinic hours, and charge premium rates for walk-in appointments. For anyone outside the major cities, or anyone who needs to see a doctor outside clinic hours, access is even more limited.</p><p>Global Health offers something different. No membership, no registration requirement, no geographic restriction. Our doctors are registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR) and offer clinical consultations in Romanian — and in other languages including english— by secure video call, same day, seven days a week, from anywhere in Romania.</p><p>Already registered with a Romanian family doctor (medic de familie)? This service works alongside your existing care — for acute issues, second opinions, or situations where you need to speak to a doctor today rather than waiting for your next available appointment.</p><h2>What We Can Help With</h2><p>Our doctors assess and manage a wide range of acute and non-urgent medical concerns, including:</p><ul><li>Respiratory &amp; ENT</li><li>Cold, flu, sinusitis, and bronchitis</li><li>COVID-19 symptoms and post-COVID concerns</li><li>Sore throat, tonsillitis, and ear infections</li><li>Chest infections and persistent cough</li><li>Mild asthma flares</li><li>Infections</li><li>Urinary tract infections — assessment and antibiotic prescription where clinically indicated</li><li>Skin infections</li><li>Respiratory infections requiring antibiotic assessment</li><li>Gastrointestinal infections</li><li>General &amp; Systemic</li><li>Fever in adults</li><li>Headaches and migraine</li><li>Nausea, vomiting, diarrhoea, and abdominal pain</li><li>Fatigue and general malaise</li><li>Skin &amp; Eyes</li><li>Rashes, eczema flares, and mild allergic reactions</li><li>Conjunctivitis and mild eye irritation</li><li>Musculoskeletal</li><li>Back pain, neck pain, and muscle strains</li><li>Minor musculoskeletal injuries</li><li>Other</li><li>Concerns about current medication or ongoing treatment</li><li>Medical letters and documentation — sick notes, referral letters</li><li>Travel health advice</li><li>Second opinions on diagnoses or treatment plans</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessmentYour doctor takes a complete history of your symptoms, relevant medical background, and current medications — and conducts a structured clinical assessment by video, to the same standard as an in-person consultation.</p><p>Clear explanation in plain EnglishYou leave your consultation knowing exactly what is happening, what to do next, and when to seek further care. Our doctors take time to answer your questions — no rushed appointment, no language barrier.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription validated in the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present this at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania. For foreign citizens without a Romanian CNP, your passport number is used — no Romanian health insurance required.</p><p>Antibiotic prescription — where clinically indicatedSince 2024, all antibiotics in Romania require a doctor's prescription. Your doctor assesses your symptoms and prescribes the appropriate antibiotic where clinically justified — including for urinary tract infections, respiratory infections, and skin infections.</p><p>Referral letters and medical documentationWhen specialist referral, blood tests, imaging, or other clinical documentation is needed, your doctor issues this on the same day.</p><p>Sick notesIf you are employed in Romania and require a sick leave certificate (concediu medical), your doctor can issue the appropriate documentation where clinically indicated. The decision to issue sick leave is always a clinical decision made by the doctor after full assessment.</p><h2>A Note on Healthcare in Romania for Expats</h2><p>Romania's public health system is structurally sound but access as a foreigner involves several practical challenges:</p><p>Registration: Access to the Romanian public health system requires registration with a family doctor (medic de familie) and enrolment with a health insurance fund. This takes time and requires documentation.</p><p>Language: While many private clinic doctors in Bucharest speak English, English-speaking primary care is rare outside the capital — and conducting a meaningful medical consultation in a language you do not speak well is genuinely difficult.</p><p>Geography: English-language private clinics are concentrated in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. For expats and international residents elsewhere in Romania, access is significantly more difficult.</p><p>Global Health removes all three barriers. CMR-registered doctors, consulting in English, available from anywhere in Romania, same day, without prior registration.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><ul><li>Expats and foreigners living in Romania without a registered Romanian family doctor</li><li>International students at Romanian universities</li><li>Digital nomads and remote workers based in Romania</li><li>Business travellers in Romania</li><li>Tourists and short-stay visitors needing same-day medical advice</li><li>Long-term residents who need to see a doctor today rather than waiting for a clinic appointment</li><li>Anyone who prefers to consult in English rather than Romanian</li></ul><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance to use this service?No. This is a private-pay service. You pay directly per consultation — no Romanian public health insurance required, no membership. Some international health insurance policies reimburse private telemedicine consultations — check with your insurer.</p><p>Can the doctor prescribe antibiotics?Yes — where clinically indicated following full assessment of your symptoms. Since 2024, all antibiotics in Romania require a doctor's prescription. Your doctor assesses your presentation and prescribes the appropriate antibiotic where the clinical evidence supports it, in accordance with antibiotic stewardship guidelines.</p><p>Can the doctor issue an electronic prescription?Yes. Our doctors are registered with the CMR and in the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). Electronic prescriptions are issued directly — you present them at any pharmacy in Romania. For foreign citizens, your passport number is used — no Romanian CNP required.</p><p>Do I need to be in Bucharest to use this service?No. This service is available from anywhere in Romania — all you need is a stable internet connection and a device with a camera.</p><p>How quickly can I get an appointment?Same-day appointments are available. Book online and your appointment is confirmed immediately.</p><p>What languages are available?English, Romanian— subject to doctor availability.</p><p>Can I get a sick note through this service?Yes. If you are employed in Romania and sick leave is clinically indicated, your doctor can issue the appropriate documentation. The decision to issue sick leave is always made by the doctor after full clinical assessment.</p><p>Is this service suitable for children?For acute paediatric concerns, this service can provide initial assessment and guidance. For complex or ongoing paediatric presentations, your doctor will advise on appropriate in-person referral.</p><p>What if my condition needs in-person care?If your doctor determines that your condition requires in-person assessment or emergency care, you will be advised immediately and directed to the appropriate service. Your safety is always the clinical priority.</p>
After

Before

detailBody

Before
<p>¿Necesita ver a un médico hoy, pero no está inscrito en la lista de un médico de familia? ¿O vive en una zona donde es difícil acceder de forma conveniente a un médico de familia? Nuestros médicos, registrados en el Colegio de Médicos de Rumanía (CMR), realizan consultas clínicas completas por videoconferencia segura, sin necesidad de ser un paciente registrado previamente, sin sala de espera y con cita el mismo día.</p><h2>Una realidad del sistema sanitario de Rumanía</h2><p>El acceso a la atención médica primaria en Rumanía es desigual. En las grandes ciudades — Bucarest, Cluj-Napoca, Timișoara, Iași — existen opciones privadas relativamente accesibles. Pero fuera de ellas, o en el caso de personas que no están inscritas en la lista de un médico de familia, obtener una consulta médica a tiempo puede ser difícil.</p><p>Global Health existe precisamente para cubrir este vacío. Nuestros médicos, registrados en el CMR, ofrecen consultas médicas por videoconferencia — en rumano, inglés— el mismo día, desde cualquier rincón de Rumanía, sin registro previo.</p><p>¿Ya tiene médico de familia? Nuestro servicio funciona de manera complementaria — para problemas agudos, una segunda opinión médica o situaciones en las que necesita hablar con un médico hoy, no en la siguiente cita disponible.</p><h2>Qué tratamos</h2><p>Nuestros médicos evalúan y manejan una amplia gama de afecciones agudas y no urgentes:</p><ul><li>Respiratorias y ORL</li><li>Resfriado, gripe, sinusitis y bronquitis</li><li>Síntomas de COVID-19 y problemas post-COVID</li><li>Dolor de garganta, amigdalitis e infecciones de oído</li><li>Infecciones pulmonares y tos persistente</li><li>Crisis leves de asma</li><li>Infecciones</li><li>Infecciones urinarias — evaluación y prescripción de antibiótico cuando esté clínicamente indicado</li><li>Infecciones de la piel</li><li>Infecciones respiratorias que requieren evaluación antibiótica</li><li>Infecciones gastrointestinales</li><li>Síntomas generales</li><li>Fiebre en adultos</li><li>Dolores de cabeza y migraña</li><li>Náuseas, vómitos, diarrea y dolor abdominal</li><li>Fatiga y malestar general</li><li>Piel y ojos</li><li>Erupciones cutáneas, eccemas y reacciones alérgicas leves</li><li>Conjuntivitis e irritaciones oculares leves</li><li>Aparato locomotor</li><li>Dolor de espalda, cuello y muscular</li><li>Traumatismos leves del aparato locomotor</li><li>Otros</li><li>Preocupaciones relacionadas con los medicamentos actuales o el tratamiento en curso</li><li>Documentos médicos — certificados médicos, cartas de derivación</li><li>Consejos de medicina del viajero</li><li>Segunda opinión médica</li></ul><h2>Qué incluye la consulta</h2><p>Evaluación clínica completaEl médico realiza una anamnesis completa de sus síntomas, su historial médico relevante y los medicamentos actuales, y lleva a cabo una evaluación clínica estructurada por videoconferencia, con el mismo estándar que una consulta presencial.</p><p>Explicación claraNuestro médico se tomará el tiempo para explicarle qué está ocurriendo, qué debe hacer a continuación y cuándo debe buscar atención adicional. La implicación del paciente en la decisión médica no es un lujo — es nuestro estándar de atención.</p><p>Receta electrónica — donde esté clínicamente indicadoCuando esté clínicamente indicado, el médico emite una receta electrónica validada en el Sistema Informático de Prescripción Electrónica (SIPE). La presenta en cualquier farmacia de Rumanía.</p><p>Prescripción de antibiótico — donde esté clínicamente indicadoA partir de 2024, todos los antibióticos en Rumanía requieren receta médica. El médico evalúa sus síntomas y prescribe el antibiótico adecuado cuando esté clínicamente justificado — incluso para infecciones urinarias, infecciones respiratorias e infecciones cutáneas — con respeto a los principios de stewardship antibiótico.</p><p>Cartas de derivación y documentación médicaCuando se necesiten derivaciones a un especialista, análisis de sangre, pruebas de imagen u otra documentación clínica, el médico las emite el mismo día.</p><p>Baja médicaSi usted está empleado en Rumanía y la baja médica está clínicamente indicada, el médico puede emitir la documentación correspondiente. La decisión de emitir la baja médica es siempre una decisión clínica tomada por el médico tras una evaluación completa.</p><h2>Por qué Global Health</h2><p>Sin registro previoNo necesita ser un paciente registrado. Reserve, consulte, obtenga un resultado clínico claro — sin burocracia, sin esperar a ser aceptado en una lista.</p><p>Médicos registrados en el CMRTodas las consultas son realizadas por médicos registrados en el Colegio de Médicos de Rumanía, conforme a las normas rumanas de telemedicina en virtud de la Ley n.º 95/2006 y del HG n.º 1133/2022.</p><p>Cita el mismo díaDisponibilidad el mismo día, siete días a la semana, incluidas tardes y fines de semana.</p><p>Accesible desde toda RumaníaNo necesita estar en Bucarest. Esté donde esté en Rumanía — solo necesita una conexión estable a internet y un dispositivo con cámara.</p><p>Tres idiomasRumano, inglés — según la disponibilidad del médico.</p><p>Receta electrónica y baja médicaNuestros médicos pueden emitir recetas electrónicas a través de SIPE y certificado de baja médica — igual que cualquier médico de familia registrado en Rumanía.</p><h2>Preguntas frecuentes</h2><p>¿Necesito tener seguro de salud rumano para utilizar este servicio?No. Este es un servicio privado, pagado directamente por consulta — no se requiere seguro público de salud rumano, ni registro, ni membresía. Algunos seguros privados cubren las consultas de telemedicina — consulte con su aseguradora.</p><p>¿Puede el médico recetar antibióticos?Sí — donde esté clínicamente indicado tras la evaluación completa de sus síntomas. A partir de 2024, todos los antibióticos en Rumanía requieren receta médica. El médico evalúa su presentación y prescribe el antibiótico adecuado cuando la evidencia clínica lo respalda, con respeto a los principios de stewardship antibiótico.</p><p>¿Puede el médico emitir una receta electrónica?Sí. Nuestros médicos están registrados en el CMR y en el Sistema Informático de Prescripción Electrónica (SIPE). Las recetas electrónicas se emiten directamente — las presenta en cualquier farmacia de Rumanía.</p><p>¿Necesito estar en Bucarest para utilizar este servicio?No. El servicio está disponible desde cualquier localidad de Rumanía — solo necesita una conexión estable a internet y un dispositivo con cámara.</p><p>¿Qué tan rápido puedo obtener una cita?Hay citas disponibles el mismo día. Reserve en línea y la cita se confirma de inmediato.</p><p>¿Qué idiomas están disponibles?Rumano, inglés— según la disponibilidad del médico.</p><p>¿Puedo obtener baja médica a través de este servicio?Sí. Si usted está empleado en Rumanía y la baja médica está clínicamente indicada, el médico puede emitir la documentación correspondiente. La decisión es siempre clínica, tomada tras una evaluación completa.</p><p>¿Es el servicio adecuado para niños?Para afecciones pediátricas agudas, el servicio puede ofrecer una evaluación inicial y orientación. Para presentaciones pediátricas complejas o continuadas, el médico recomendará derivación para una evaluación presencial.</p><p>¿Qué ocurre si mi afección requiere atención presencial?Si el médico determina que su afección requiere evaluación presencial o atención de urgencia, se le informará de inmediato y se le derivará al servicio correspondiente. Su seguridad es siempre la prioridad clínica.</p>
After
