serviceTranslations / cmr9d1jn00004vcjuo3auwezw EN

Update only the following fields; preserve the rest of this row.

seoTitle

Before
Mental Health Online | Romania | English Doctor | Anxiety & Depression
After
General mental health assessment | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Confidential support for anxiety, depression, burnout, and other mental health concerns. Our CMR-registered doctors offer confidential GP-level mental health assessments in English. Same-day appointments available in Romania.
After
General mental health assessment. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Mental Health Assessment — Online, in English
After
General mental health assessment

detailBody

Before
<p>Mental health difficulties — anxiety, depression, burnout, adjustment to a new country, or persistent patterns of thought and behaviour that are affecting your quality of life — respond best to structured, evidence-based clinical support from a qualified professional.</p><p>For expats and international residents in Romania, the barrier to mental health support is frequently linguistic. Finding a therapist or psychiatrist who works at a level of English that allows genuine clinical depth is genuinely difficult — particularly outside Bucharest. Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer confidential GP-level mental health assessments in English by secure video call — removing the language barrier that prevents many international residents from accessing the support they need.</p><h2>What This Service Is — And What It Is Not</h2><p>Understanding which level of mental health support is right for you helps ensure you access the most appropriate care.</p><p>This GP-level mental health consultation is appropriate for:</p><ul><li>Initial assessment of anxiety, low mood, depression, and stress</li><li>Screening for common mental health conditions using validated clinical tools</li><li>Assessment of how mental health symptoms interact with physical health</li><li>Clinical recommendations and care planning at the doctor's professional discretion</li><li>Referral for psychology, psychiatry, or other specialist support where indicated</li><li>Guidance on mental health services available in Romania in English</li><li>Review of existing mental health management and ongoing support</li><li>ADHD — initial screening and referral pathway where indicated</li></ul><p>This service is not appropriate for:</p><ul><li>Crisis intervention — if you are in crisis, use the resources at the top of this page</li><li>Psychiatric assessment — if you need a psychiatrist, your doctor coordinates referral</li><li>Psychotherapy or counselling — this is a medical assessment, not a therapy session</li></ul><p>Assessment of psychosis, severe bipolar disorder, or complex psychiatric conditions — these require specialist psychiatric assessment</p><ul><li>Involuntary psychiatric assessment or treatment under Romanian law</li></ul><p>Not sure which level of support is right for you? Start with this consultation. Your doctor assesses your presentation and advises on the most appropriate next steps — including whether specialist psychology or psychiatry is indicated.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Anxiety and stress</li><li>Generalised anxiety disorder</li><li>Social anxiety and performance anxiety</li><li>Health anxiety</li><li>Panic attacks and panic disorder</li><li>Work-related stress and burnout</li><li>Adjustment difficulties — new country, new job, relationship changes</li><li>Expat-specific stress — cultural adjustment, isolation, identity concerns, family separation</li><li>Mood</li><li>Low mood and depression</li><li>Persistent sadness and loss of interest</li><li>Emotional exhaustion and anhedonia</li><li>Postnatal mood changes — low mood and anxiety after childbirth</li><li>Seasonal affective disorder</li><li>Grief and bereavement</li><li>Sleep and behaviour</li><li>Insomnia and sleep disturbance with psychological contributors</li><li>Fatigue with psychological contributors</li><li>Appetite changes and eating patterns related to mood</li><li>Concentration and cognitive difficulties related to anxiety or depression</li><li>Trauma and life events</li></ul><p>Initial assessment of trauma-related symptoms — with referral to specialist psychological support where indicated</p><ul><li>Adjustment to significant life events — bereavement, relationship breakdown, job loss</li><li>Expat stress and relocation — cultural adjustment, isolation, identity concerns</li><li>Ongoing mental health management</li><li>Review of existing mental health conditions managed at GP level</li><li>Mental health assessment in the context of chronic disease management</li><li>ADHD — initial screening and referral for formal assessment where indicated</li><li>Assessment of mental health medication concerns at GP level</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Confidential clinical assessmentYour doctor conducts a structured and comprehensive mental health assessment — covering your current symptoms, mood, sleep, energy, appetite, concentration, relationships, work or academic pressures, medical history, and any previous mental health support. The conversation is guided, confidential, and non-judgmental.</p><p>Validated screening toolsWhere appropriate, your doctor uses validated clinical screening tools — including PHQ-9 for depression and GAD-7 for anxiety — ensuring a structured, evidence-based assessment of your current mental health.</p><p>Safety assessmentEvery mental health consultation includes a structured safety assessment — conducted with sensitivity as a standard component of clinical care. If risk is identified, your doctor advises on the appropriate pathway and ensures you are connected to the right support.</p><p>Personalised care planBased on the assessment, your doctor advises on the most appropriate next steps — which may include wellbeing and lifestyle recommendations, referral for psychology or psychotherapy, guidance on community and specialist services available in Romania, psychiatric referral where indicated, or clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion.</p><p>Navigation supportNavigating the mental health system in Romania as an international resident is genuinely difficult. Your doctor provides clear guidance on what services are available in English, how to access them, and what to expect from the Romanian mental health system.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated at GP level, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through SIPE. You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain psychiatric medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><h2>Mental Health Support for Expats in Romania</h2><p>Mental health difficulties are disproportionately common among expats and international residents — driven by isolation, cultural adjustment, language barriers, family separation, and the cumulative stress of building a life in a new country.</p><p>Accessing mental health support in Romania as an international resident brings additional challenges. English-speaking psychiatrists and psychologists exist in Bucharest and in some other major cities — but availability is limited, waiting times can be long, and the cost of private mental health care in Romania is significant. For expats outside Bucharest, the options narrow further.</p><p>Our service provides an accessible first step — a structured, clinical mental health assessment in English, same day, from anywhere in Romania — with clear guidance on next steps and how to access specialist support where needed.</p><h2>Confidentiality</h2><p>Everything discussed during your mental health consultation is confidential and protected under Romanian and European data protection law — including Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and Romanian Law no. 190/2018 on personal data processing.</p><p>There are two circumstances in which confidentiality may need to be carefully considered — when there is serious and immediate risk to your life or safety, or when there is serious risk to the safety of another person. Your doctor discusses confidentiality with you at the outset of the consultation.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Is this a counselling or therapy service?No. This is a GP-level mental health assessment. Our doctors assess your current mental health, provide clinical recommendations, and refer you to the appropriate level of support — including counselling, psychotherapy, or psychiatry where indicated. Counselling or psychotherapy sessions are not provided through this service.</p><p>What if I need to see a psychiatrist?If your assessment indicates that psychiatric assessment is needed, your doctor advises clearly and coordinates referral to the appropriate specialist service — including English-speaking psychiatric services in Romania where available.</p><p>Will my employer or anyone else be informed?No. Your consultation is completely confidential and protected under Romanian and European data protection law. Information is not shared without your explicit consent, except in the very limited circumstances described in the confidentiality section above.</p><p>Can I use this service if I am already seeing a therapist?Yes. A GP-level mental health assessment can complement ongoing psychological or therapeutic support — particularly where clinical recommendations, physical health assessment, or care coordination are needed alongside your therapeutic work.</p><p>Is this service suitable for adolescents?Mental health consultations for patients under 16 require parental or guardian presence and consent. For adolescents aged 16 and over, the doctor assesses capacity to consent individually in accordance with Romanian medical and legal standards.</p><p>What if I am in crisis during or after the consultation?If you are in crisis, use the crisis resources listed at the top of this page — Telefonul Verde (0800 800 381) or 112 in immediate danger. This service is not a crisis intervention service.</p><p>Can I discuss ADHD concerns through this service?Yes. Initial ADHD screening at GP level is available through this service. Formal ADHD diagnosis requires specialist assessment — your doctor advises on the appropriate referral pathway if screening indicates this is warranted.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance?No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions issued through this service are dispensed at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p><p>I am not in Bucharest — can I still use this service?Yes. This service is available from anywhere in Romania — all you need is a stable internet connection and a device with a camera.</p>
After

serviceTranslations / cmr9d1qoi000cvcjuevb0bv4t EN

Update only the following fields; preserve the rest of this row.

seoTitle

Before
Men's Health Online | Romania | Confidential English Doctor
After
Men’s health consultation | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Confidential consultations for erectile dysfunction, testosterone, libido, and men's health. Our CMR-registered doctors offer confidential assessments in English by secure video call. Same-day appointments available.
After
Men’s health consultation. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Men's Health — Confidential Online Assessment in English
After
Men’s health consultation

detailBody

Before
<p>Men's health concerns are consistently under-reported across all populations — not because men do not experience them, but because the combination of stigma, time pressure, and access barriers makes seeking help feel difficult. A confidential video consultation with a CMR-registered doctor removes several of these barriers simultaneously: no waiting room, no need to explain yourself to a receptionist, no travel to a clinic, and — for those who prefer it — the option to consult in English, Romanian.</p><p>They should. Because in the vast majority of cases, these are medical conditions with medical causes — and medical solutions.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer confidential men's health assessments by secure video call in English. No waiting room, no language barrier, no discomfort of an in-person consultation for a sensitive concern. Just a full clinical assessment and a clear plan, same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adult men with:</p><ul><li>Erectile dysfunction — new onset or worsening</li><li>Reduced libido or loss of interest in sexual activity</li><li>Premature or delayed ejaculation</li><li>Performance anxiety with clinical impact</li><li>Symptoms of testosterone deficiency — fatigue, mood changes, reduced muscle mass, weight gain</li><li>Hormonal imbalance affecting energy, mood, weight, or sexual function</li><li>Sexual dysfunction related to diabetes, cardiovascular disease, or medication</li></ul><p>General men's health concerns — cardiovascular risk, metabolic health, hair loss with hormonal basis</p><p>A need for investigation requests — testosterone panel, cardiovascular risk assessment, metabolic profile</p><h2>Why Erectile Dysfunction Deserves Clinical Assessment</h2><p>Erectile dysfunction is not simply a lifestyle inconvenience. It is one of the most reliable early warning signs of underlying cardiovascular disease — often presenting years before a cardiac event. It is also strongly associated with diabetes, hypertension, testosterone deficiency, and medication side effects.</p><p>A man who treats erectile dysfunction as an embarrassing inconvenience rather than a clinical signal is missing an important opportunity for early intervention on conditions that, left unaddressed, carry serious long-term health consequences.</p><p>The clinical history is the most powerful diagnostic tool in men's health — and a full history can be taken by video call. Our doctors assess the complete clinical picture, not just the presenting symptom.</p><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessmentYour doctor reviews your symptoms in detail, medical history, current medications, cardiovascular risk factors, hormonal indicators, psychological contributors, and lifestyle factors — building a complete clinical picture of what is causing your symptoms.</p><p>Identification of underlying causesMen's health symptoms rarely exist in isolation. Erectile dysfunction, for example, is frequently an early marker of cardiovascular disease, diabetes, or testosterone deficiency. Your doctor assesses the complete clinical picture — not just the presenting complaint.</p><p>Clinical management planBased on the assessment, your doctor advises on appropriate next steps — which may include lifestyle and metabolic optimisation, investigation requests for blood tests or hormonal assessment, clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion, or referral to urology or endocrinology where indicated.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain controlled medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><p>Referral and investigation coordinationWhen specialist referral or blood tests are clinically indicated, your doctor issues the relevant documentation on the same day.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Sexual health and function</li><li>Erectile dysfunction — assessment of medical, vascular, hormonal, and psychological factors</li><li>Premature and delayed ejaculation</li><li>Reduced libido and loss of interest in sexual activity</li><li>Performance anxiety with clinical impact</li><li>Sexual dysfunction related to diabetes, cardiovascular disease, or medication</li><li>Hormonal and metabolic</li><li>Testosterone deficiency — symptoms, assessment, and management pathway</li><li>Hormonal imbalance affecting energy, mood, weight, and sexual function</li><li>Metabolic syndrome and its impact on men's health</li><li>Weight-related hormonal disruption</li><li>General men's health</li><li>Cardiovascular risk assessment in men</li><li>Fatigue, low energy, and mood changes with hormonal or metabolic cause</li><li>Hair loss with hormonal basis</li><li>Prostate health concerns and referral where indicated</li><li>General health assessments and preventive care for men</li></ul><h2>Confidentiality</h2><p>Everything discussed during your consultation is confidential and protected under Romanian and European data protection law — including Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and Romanian Law no. 190/2018 on personal data processing.</p><p>Your consultation is conducted by encrypted video call. Nobody at your workplace, in your household, or in your social circle needs to know you booked an appointment. Your health records are protected under strict data protection standards. Information is not shared without your explicit consent, except in the limited circumstances required by law.</p><h2>Men's Health in Romania — Why This Service</h2><p>Men's health concerns are consistently under-reported across all populations — but for expats and international residents in Romania, there is an additional barrier: navigating a sensitive conversation about sexual health or hormonal symptoms in a language that is not your own, with a doctor you have never met before, in a system you do not fully understand.</p><p>Our service removes all of these barriers. A CMR-registered doctor, consulting in English, available from anywhere in Romania, same day, with complete clinical confidentiality.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Is this a prescription service?No. This is a clinical assessment service. Our doctors conduct a full clinical assessment and advise on appropriate management based on clinical findings. Any clinical recommendations — including prescriptions where clinically indicated — are made at the doctor's professional discretion following full assessment.</p><p>Can erectile dysfunction really be assessed by video call?Yes — for the vast majority of presentations. The clinical history is the most powerful diagnostic tool in men's health and a full history can be taken by video call. Your doctor also assesses lifestyle factors, cardiovascular risk, hormonal indicators, and psychological contributors during the call. Where physical examination findings are needed that cannot be assessed remotely, your doctor advises on appropriate in-person assessment.</p><p>What blood tests might be needed?Depending on your presentation, your doctor may request a testosterone panel, cardiovascular risk assessment, fasting glucose, HbA1c, thyroid function, and metabolic profile. Investigation requests can be coordinated in the same consultation.</p><p>Is this service suitable for younger men?Yes. Erectile dysfunction and other men's health concerns can arise in men from their twenties — often with psychological or lifestyle causes at younger ages. This service is appropriate for adult men of all ages.</p><p>What if my condition requires specialist input?If your assessment indicates that specialist input is needed — urology, endocrinology, or cardiology — your doctor advises clearly and coordinates the referral on the same day.</p><p>Can I use this service if I do not have a Romanian family doctor?Yes. This service is available to all men in Romania — including expats and international residents without a registered Romanian family doctor.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance?No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions issued through this service are dispensed at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p>
After
<p>Men's health concerns are consistently under-reported across all populations — not because men do not experience them, but because the combination of stigma, time pressure, and access barriers makes seeking help feel difficult. A confidential video consultation with a CMR-registered doctor removes several of these barriers simultaneously: no waiting room, no need to explain yourself to a receptionist, no travel to a clinic, and — for those who prefer it — the option to consult in English, Romanian.</p><p>They should. Because in the vast majority of cases, these are medical conditions with medical causes — and medical solutions.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer confidential men's health assessments by secure video call in English. No waiting room, no language barrier, no discomfort of an in-person consultation for a sensitive concern. Just a full clinical assessment and a clear plan, same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for adult men with:</p><ul><li>Erectile dysfunction — new onset or worsening</li><li>Reduced libido or loss of interest in sexual activity</li><li>Premature or delayed ejaculation</li><li>Performance anxiety with clinical impact</li><li>Symptoms of testosterone deficiency — fatigue, mood changes, reduced muscle mass, weight gain</li><li>Hormonal imbalance affecting energy, mood, weight, or sexual function</li><li>Sexual dysfunction related to diabetes, cardiovascular disease, or medication</li></ul><p>General men's health concerns — cardiovascular risk, metabolic health, hair loss with hormonal basis</p><p>A need for investigation requests — testosterone panel, cardiovascular risk assessment, metabolic profile</p><h2>Why Erectile Dysfunction Deserves Clinical Assessment</h2><p>Erectile dysfunction is not simply a lifestyle inconvenience. It is one of the most reliable early warning signs of underlying cardiovascular disease — often presenting years before a cardiac event. It is also strongly associated with diabetes, hypertension, testosterone deficiency, and medication side effects.</p><p>A man who treats erectile dysfunction as an embarrassing inconvenience rather than a clinical signal is missing an important opportunity for early intervention on conditions that, left unaddressed, carry serious long-term health consequences.</p><p>The clinical history is the most powerful diagnostic tool in men's health — and a full history can be taken by video call. Our doctors assess the complete clinical picture, not just the presenting symptom.</p><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessmentYour doctor reviews your symptoms in detail, medical history, current medications, cardiovascular risk factors, hormonal indicators, psychological contributors, and lifestyle factors — building a complete clinical picture of what is causing your symptoms.</p><p>Identification of underlying causesMen's health symptoms rarely exist in isolation. Erectile dysfunction, for example, is frequently an early marker of cardiovascular disease, diabetes, or testosterone deficiency. Your doctor assesses the complete clinical picture — not just the presenting complaint.</p><p>Clinical management planBased on the assessment, your doctor advises on appropriate next steps — which may include lifestyle and metabolic optimisation, investigation requests for blood tests or hormonal assessment, clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion, or referral to urology or endocrinology where indicated.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain controlled medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law. Your doctor advises if this applies to your situation.</p><p>Referral and investigation coordinationWhen specialist referral or blood tests are clinically indicated, your doctor issues the relevant documentation on the same day.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Sexual health and function</li><li>Erectile dysfunction — assessment of medical, vascular, hormonal, and psychological factors</li><li>Premature and delayed ejaculation</li><li>Reduced libido and loss of interest in sexual activity</li><li>Performance anxiety with clinical impact</li><li>Sexual dysfunction related to diabetes, cardiovascular disease, or medication</li><li>Hormonal and metabolic</li><li>Testosterone deficiency — symptoms, assessment, and management pathway</li><li>Hormonal imbalance affecting energy, mood, weight, and sexual function</li><li>Metabolic syndrome and its impact on men's health</li><li>Weight-related hormonal disruption</li><li>General men's health</li><li>Cardiovascular risk assessment in men</li><li>Fatigue, low energy, and mood changes with hormonal or metabolic cause</li><li>Hair loss with hormonal basis</li><li>Prostate health concerns and referral where indicated</li><li>General health assessments and preventive care for men</li></ul><h2>Confidentiality</h2><p>Everything discussed during your consultation is confidential and protected under Romanian and European data protection law — including Regulation (EU) 2016/679 (GDPR) and Romanian Law no. 190/2018 on personal data processing.</p><p>Your consultation is conducted by encrypted video call. Nobody at your workplace, in your household, or in your social circle needs to know you booked an appointment. Your health records are protected under strict data protection standards. Information is not shared without your explicit consent, except in the limited circumstances required by law.</p><h2>Men's Health in Romania — Why This Service</h2><p>Men's health concerns are consistently under-reported across all populations — but for expats and international residents in Romania, there is an additional barrier: navigating a sensitive conversation about sexual health or hormonal symptoms in a language that is not your own, with a doctor you have never met before, in a system you do not fully understand.</p><p>Our service removes all of these barriers. A CMR-registered doctor, consulting in English, available from anywhere in Romania, same day, with complete clinical confidentiality.</p>

seoTitle

Before
Women's Health Online | Romania | English Doctor | Same Day
After
Women’s health consultation | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Confidential consultations for contraception, hormonal health, menopause, and women's health. Our CMR-registered doctors offer confidential assessments in English by secure video call. Same-day appointments available.
After
Women’s health consultation. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Women's Health — Confidential Online Assessment in English
After
Women’s health consultation

detailBody

Before
<p>Romania's healthcare system allows women to access gynaecology directly — without a GP referral. In practice, English-speaking gynaecologists are concentrated in a small number of private clinics in major cities. But access barriers exist for Romanian nationals too — particularly outside Bucharest, and for anyone without a registered family doctor who wants a structured women's health assessment before deciding whether to pursue specialist gynaecological care.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer confidential women's health consultations in English by secure video call. Whether you need contraception advice, hormonal assessment, menopause support, or a women's health concern addressed without a language barrier — we are here, same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for women who:</p><ul><li>Need contraception advice, review, or continuation in English</li><li>Have hormonal health concerns — PCOS, thyroid conditions, perimenopause, or menopause</li></ul><p>Are experiencing symptoms they believe may have a hormonal or metabolic basis — fatigue, mood changes, weight gain, skin changes, hair loss</p><ul><li>Need assessment of menstrual irregularities, heavy periods, or painful periods</li><li>Have sexual health concerns — reduced libido, vaginal dryness, or pain during intercourse</li><li>Are pregnant or planning pregnancy and need preconception advice</li><li>Need urinary tract infection assessment and management</li><li>Have received results from a Romanian gynaecologist that they want explained in English</li><li>Are expats or international residents who need women's healthcare in a language they understand</li><li>Do not have a registered Romanian family doctor or gynaecologist</li></ul><h2>Women's Healthcare in Romania — What Expats Need to Know</h2><p>Romania's healthcare system allows women to access gynaecology directly — without a GP referral. In practice, English-speaking gynaecologists are concentrated in Bucharest and a small number of private clinics in other major cities. For expats outside these areas, or for women who prefer to discuss women's health concerns in English before deciding whether to pursue specialist gynaecological care, access is genuinely limited.</p><p>Global Health provides GP-level women's health consultations in English — covering hormonal health, contraception, menopause, and general women's health — complementing, rather than replacing, specialist gynaecological care. When specialist gynaecological assessment is needed, your doctor coordinates the referral on the same day.</p><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessment Your doctor reviews your symptoms in detail, menstrual and reproductive history, current medications, hormonal indicators, relevant medical history, and lifestyle factors — building a complete clinical picture of your concern.</p><p>Hormonal and metabolic assessment Women's health symptoms frequently have hormonal or metabolic roots that go unaddressed in standard acute consultations. Your doctor assesses hormonal contributors — thyroid function, insulin resistance, PCOS, and perimenopause — where clinically relevant.</p><p>Clinical management plan Based on the assessment, your doctor advises on the most appropriate next steps — which may include lifestyle and nutritional recommendations, clinical recommendations at the doctor's professional discretion, referral for blood tests or specialist assessment, or ongoing management at GP level.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriate When clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription through the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present it at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania.</p><p>Note: Special-regime prescriptions covering certain controlled medications cannot be transmitted electronically under Romanian law.</p><p>Referral and investigation coordination When gynaecological referral, blood tests, or other investigations are clinically indicated, your doctor issues the relevant documentation on the same day.</p><h2>Conditions Commonly Assessed</h2><ul><li>Contraception and reproductive health</li></ul><p>Contraception assessment and review — combined pill, mini-pill, patch, ring, injectable, and non-hormonal options</p><ul><li>Side effects related to contraception and management</li><li>Preconception advice and planning</li><li>Fertility concerns — initial assessment and referral where indicated</li><li>Hormonal health</li><li>Polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) — assessment, management support, and monitoring</li><li>Endometriosis — symptom management and referral coordination</li><li>Premenstrual syndrome (PMS) and premenstrual dysphoric disorder (PMDD)</li><li>Irregular, heavy, or painful periods</li><li>Hormonal imbalance affecting mood, weight, energy, and skin</li><li>Thyroid conditions — particularly relevant for women's hormonal health</li><li>Menopause and perimenopause</li><li>Perimenopause assessment and symptom management</li><li>Menopause — hot flushes, sleep disturbance, mood changes, cognitive symptoms</li></ul><p>Hormone replacement therapy (HRT) — assessment, initiation, and ongoing review at the doctor's professional discretion</p><ul><li>Post-menopause health and long-term wellbeing</li><li>Sexual health</li><li>Reduced libido and hormonal contributors</li><li>Vaginal dryness and discomfort — assessment and management</li><li>Pain during intercourse — clinical assessment and referral where indicated</li><li>General women's health</li><li>Urinary tract infections and recurrent UTIs</li><li>Breast health concerns and referral where indicated</li><li>Cervical screening queries and referral coordination</li><li>Skin and hair changes with hormonal basis — acne, hair loss, hirsutism</li><li>Mental health in women — anxiety, depression, and postnatal mood concerns</li><li>Weight management with hormonal or metabolic basis</li></ul><h2>Termination of Pregnancy — Important Note</h2><p>Termination of pregnancy in Romania requires in-person attendance at a licensed medical facility. This cannot be arranged through a telemedicine consultation and is not available through this service. If you need information on services available in Romania, your doctor can provide appropriate guidance and referral information during your consultation.</p><h2>Why Hormonal Health Is Frequently Missed</h2><p>Many symptoms that women attribute to stress or age — fatigue, mood changes, weight gain, skin and hair changes — have specific hormonal or metabolic causes that standard acute consultations do not investigate. PCOS affects approximately one in ten women and is frequently undiagnosed. Perimenopause symptoms are routinely attributed to anxiety. Hormonal causes of fatigue, weight, and mood are systematically underassessed in standard primary care.</p><p>A consultation focused specifically on women's health — with time, hormonal literacy, and a doctor who assesses symptoms in the complete context of women's health rather than just the acute presenting complaint — is clinically more effective than a generic GP appointment. This is what this service offers.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Can I access contraception through this service? Yes. Contraception assessment and review is a central part of this service. Your doctor conducts a full clinical assessment and advises on the most appropriate options based on your medical history and individual needs. Clinical recommendations are made at the doctor's professional discretion following assessment.</p><p>Can I access HRT through this service? HRT can be assessed and initiated at GP level where clinically appropriate. Your doctor conducts a full assessment of your symptoms, medical history, and suitability before advising on any hormonal management. All recommendations are made at the doctor's professional discretion.</p><p>Do I need a gynaecologist referral to use this service? No. This service is available directly — no referral needed. If your assessment indicates that specialist gynaecological input is needed, your doctor coordinates the referral on the same day.</p><p>Can you explain results I received from a Romanian gynaecologist? Yes. If you have received results, letters, or a treatment plan from a Romanian gynaecologist and want them reviewed and explained clearly, bring them to your consultation. Your doctor reviews them in full and explains the clinical findings and recommendations clearly.</p><p>Is this service suitable for pregnancy-related concerns? For general preconception advice and early pregnancy questions, yes. For ongoing antenatal care, obstetric concerns, or anything requiring in-person obstetric assessment, your doctor advises on appropriate referral.</p><p>Does this service include termination of pregnancy? No. Termination of pregnancy in Romania requires in-person attendance at a licensed medical facility and cannot be arranged through a telemedicine consultation.</p><p>Can I use this service if I do not have a Romanian family doctor or gynaecologist? Yes. This service is specifically designed for women — including expats and international residents — who do not have a registered Romanian family doctor or gynaecologist but need access to women's healthcare in English.</p><p>Do I need Romanian health insurance? No. This is a private-pay service. Prescriptions issued through this service are dispensed at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p>
After

In practice, English-speaking gynaecologists are concentrated in a small number of private clinics in major cities. But access barriers exist for Romanian nationals too — particularly outside Bucharest, and for anyone without a registered family doctor who wants a structured women's health assessment before deciding whether to pursue specialist gynaecological care.</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer confidential women's health consultations in English by secure video call. Whether you need contraception advice, hormonal assessment, menopause support, or a women's health concern addressed without a language barrier — we are here, same day, from anywhere in Romania.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for women who:</p><ul><li>Need contraception advice, review, or continuation in English</li><li>Have hormonal health concerns — PCOS, thyroid conditions, perimenopause, or menopause</li></ul><p>Are experiencing symptoms they believe may have a hormonal or metabolic basis — fatigue, mood changes, weight gain, skin changes, hair loss</p><ul><li>Need assessment of menstrual irregularities, heavy periods, or painful periods</li><li>Have sexual health concerns — reduced libido, vaginal dryness, or pain during intercourse</li><li>Are pregnant or planning pregnancy and need preconception advice</li><li>Need urinary tract infection assessment and management</li><li>Have received results from a Romanian gynaecologist that they want explained in English</li><li>Are expats or international residents who need women's healthcare in a language they understand</li><li>Do not have a registered Romanian family doctor or gynaecologist</li></ul><h2>Women's Healthcare in Romania — What Expats Need to Know</h2><p>Romania's healthcare system allows women to access gynaecology directly — without a GP referral. serviceTranslations / cmr9d1iqv0003vcjuioj401i0 EN

Update only the following fields; preserve the rest of this row.

seoTitle

Before
Referral Letters & Investigations Online | Romania | CMR Doctor
After
Tests and referral advice | Romania

seoDescription

Before
Referral letters, blood tests, and imaging requests for Romania. Our CMR-registered doctors issue clinical documentation after full assessment. Same-day appointments available anywhere in Romania.
After
Tests and referral advice. Consultations in Romanian and English. Check the doctor and available times before booking.

heroTitle

Before
Referral Letters & Investigations — Clinical Documentation Online
After
Tests and referral advice

detailBody

Before
<p>Need to see a specialist but do not have a referral letter? Need blood tests or imaging ordered by a doctor? Received results from a Romanian specialist that you want reviewed and explained clearly before deciding how to proceed?</p><p>Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), issue clinical referral letters, blood test requests, and imaging requests following a full clinical assessment — giving you the documentation you need to access private specialist care and diagnostic investigations in Romania. Same day, from anywhere in the country, in English, Romanian.</p><h2>How Specialist Access Works in Romania</h2><p>Romania has a dual-track referral system — one for the public system and one for private care.</p><p>Public system specialist access requires a referral from a registered family doctor (medic de familie) contracted with CNAS. Without this referral, public system specialist appointments are not reimbursed. Global Health cannot provide public system referrals — this requires a registered family doctor contracted with the Romanian public health insurance system.</p><p>Private specialist access is available directly in most cases — you can book a private specialist appointment without a referral letter. However arriving at a specialist appointment without clinical context significantly reduces the quality of that first consultation. A specialist who receives a clear referral letter — describing your symptoms, relevant history, current medications, and the specific clinical question — conducts a more focused and efficient assessment.</p><p>This service covers:</p><ul><li>Private referral letters — for private specialist consultations of your choice</li><li>Blood test requests — for private laboratory testing at collection points across Romania</li><li>Imaging requests — for private X-ray, ultrasound, MRI, or CT</li><li>Clinical summary letters — documenting your assessment and findings for continuity of care</li></ul><p>Results review — blood test results, imaging reports, or specialist letters reviewed and explained clearly</p><p>This service does not cover:</p><ul><li>Public system specialist referrals — these require a family doctor contracted with CNAS</li><li>Emergency referrals — call 112 or go to the nearest emergency department immediately</li></ul><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><p>This consultation is appropriate for:</p><p>Anyone who wants to see a private specialist in Romania and needs a clinical referral letter to accompany the appointment</p><ul><li>Patients who need blood tests or imaging ordered by a doctor before booking privately</li></ul><p>Patients who have received results — from blood tests, imaging, or specialist consultations — that they want reviewed and explained clearly</p><p>Patients with a specific clinical concern they want formally assessed and documented before pursuing specialist care</p><p>Patients who want a clinical summary letter — for insurance purposes, for a new doctor, or for continuity of care</p><p>Patients who received a clinical letter or results in Romanian they want reviewed and contextualised</p><p>Anyone in Romania without a registered family doctor who needs clinical documentation to access private specialist care</p><h2>Why a Referral Letter Adds Value</h2><p>Direct access to private specialists in Romania is available in most cases without a referral letter. But a specialist who sees you without clinical context must start from scratch — and consultation time is spent gathering information that could have been communicated in writing in advance.</p><p>A referral letter issued after clinical assessment contains everything the specialist needs before you walk in — your symptoms, their duration and severity, your current medications, relevant investigations already completed, and the specific clinical question you are asking the specialist to address. The specialist can immediately focus on the clinical problem rather than taking a basic history.</p><p>The difference in the quality of that first specialist appointment — between arriving with a comprehensive referral letter versus arriving cold — is significant.</p><h2>Referral Letters — What They Include</h2><p>A referral letter issued by a Global Health doctor following clinical assessment includes:</p><ul><li>Patient details and relevant medical history</li></ul><p>Description of the presenting concern — symptoms, duration, severity, and relevant clinical findings from the video consultation</p><ul><li>Current medications and relevant investigations already completed</li><li>Specific clinical question for the specialist — what you are asking them to assess or advise on</li><li>Degree of urgency — routine, soon, or urgent — based on clinical assessment</li></ul><p>The letter is issued in the language appropriate for the specialist — Romanian for Romanian-speaking specialists, English where needed. Discuss your preference with your doctor during the consultation.</p><h2>Blood Tests — What Can Be Requested</h2><p>Following clinical assessment, your doctor can issue blood test requests for a wide range of investigations, including:</p><ul><li>Metabolic and cardiovascular</li><li>Full blood count (FBC / hemoleucograma)</li><li>Fasting glucose and HbA1c</li><li>Lipid panel — total cholesterol, LDL, HDL, triglycerides</li><li>Renal function — creatinine, eGFR, urea, electrolytes</li><li>Liver function tests</li><li>Thyroid function — TSH, free T4, free T3</li><li>Uric acid</li><li>Ferritin, iron studies, and B12/folate</li><li>Hormonal and reproductive</li><li>Testosterone panel — total and free testosterone, LH, FSH, SHBG</li><li>Female hormonal panel — oestradiol, FSH, LH, progesterone, AMH</li><li>Thyroid antibodies — anti-TPO, anti-thyroglobulin</li><li>Cortisol</li><li>Prolactin</li><li>DHEA-S</li><li>Inflammatory and autoimmune</li><li>CRP and ESR (VSH)</li><li>Rheumatoid factor and anti-CCP</li><li>ANA and ANCA panels</li><li>Complement levels</li><li>Infectious disease</li><li>HIV, hepatitis B, hepatitis C serology</li><li>STI panels</li><li>Post-vaccination immunity checks — hepatitis B, MMR</li><li>Other</li><li>Vitamin D</li><li>Coeliac screen — anti-tTG IgA</li><li>PSA — prostate specific antigen</li><li>HCG — pregnancy test</li><li>Allergy panels — specific IgE</li></ul><p>Blood test requests are issued following clinical assessment — your doctor advises on which tests are clinically indicated for your specific presentation.</p><h2>Accessing Private Blood Tests in Romania</h2><p>Private blood test collection points are widely available across Romania — in Bucharest and in major regional cities including Cluj-Napoca, Timișoara, Iași, Brașov, and Constanța. Private laboratory networks operate throughout the country with collection points in most towns.</p><p>After your consultation, your doctor issues the investigation request and advises on where to go for collection based on your location. You book the collection appointment directly — no Romanian public health insurance is required for private blood tests. Results are typically available within 24 to 72 hours depending on the tests requested, though some specialist panels may take longer.</p><h2>Imaging Requests</h2><p>Where X-ray, ultrasound, MRI, or CT is clinically indicated following assessment, your doctor issues a formal imaging request. Private radiology is widely accessible in Romania — major cities and most regional centres have private radiology facilities that accept direct booking with a doctor's request letter.</p><p>Your doctor advises on the most appropriate imaging modality for your specific clinical question and the urgency of the investigation.</p><h2>Results Review</h2><p>Received blood test results, imaging reports, or specialist letters that you want reviewed and explained clearly? Your doctor reviews your results in full during a follow-up consultation — explaining the clinical findings, what they mean for your health, what the next steps are, and whether further investigation or specialist input is needed.</p><p>This is particularly useful for patients who have received results or clinical letters in Romanian and want them explained clearly — whether they are Romanian nationals who want more time to discuss results than a standard clinic appointment allows, or international residents who need the clinical content explained in English.</p><h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2><p>Can I get a referral letter without a full clinical consultation?No. Referral letters are issued following a full clinical assessment. Your doctor needs to assess your symptoms and history to write a clinically meaningful referral letter. A referral letter without clinical assessment would not add value to your specialist appointment.</p><p>Can this service provide public system specialist referrals?No. Public system specialist referrals in Romania require a family doctor contracted with CNAS. Global Health provides private referral letters for private specialist access.</p><p>Does the referral letter need to be in Romanian?For Romanian-speaking specialists, yes — the referral letter is issued in Romanian. For international specialists or English-speaking providers, the letter can be issued in English. Your doctor issues it in the appropriate language based on who you are seeing — discuss this during the consultation.</p><p>How quickly can I get blood test results?Turnaround times vary by test — most standard blood tests are available within 24 to 72 hours from private collection points in Romania. Some specialist panels take longer. Your doctor advises on expected turnaround when issuing the request.</p><p>Can I use this service if I have Romanian public health insurance?Yes — but this service covers private referrals and private investigation requests. Your Romanian public health insurance covers reimbursed specialist referrals only when issued by a family doctor contracted with CNAS. Private blood tests and private specialist appointments booked through this service are at full private cost regardless of insurance status.</p><p>What if my results show something serious?If your results review reveals findings requiring urgent attention, your doctor advises immediately on the appropriate next steps — including whether emergency care is needed. If emergency care is needed, call 112 or go to the nearest emergency department immediately.</p><p>Can I use this service without a registered family doctor?Yes. This service is available to anyone in Romania — no prior registration, no family doctor required, no health insurance needed.</p><p>Can I use this service from anywhere in Romania?Yes. This service is available from anywhere in Romania — all you need is a stable internet connection and a device with a camera.</p>
After

detailBody

Before
<p>Need to see a doctor today — in English, without a Romanian GP registration? Our doctors, registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR), offer full clinical assessments by secure video call. No waiting rooms, no language barrier, no need to already be registered with a Romanian family doctor.</p><p>Whether you are an expat, international student, business traveller, digital nomad, or anyone in Romania who needs to speak to a doctor today — we are here, same day, from anywhere in the country.</p><h2>Why Expats Choose This Service</h2><p>Finding English-speaking primary care in Romania is genuinely difficult outside a handful of private clinics concentrated in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. Most require prior registration, operate only during weekday clinic hours, and charge premium rates for walk-in appointments. For anyone outside the major cities, or anyone who needs to see a doctor outside clinic hours, access is even more limited.</p><p>Global Health offers something different. No membership, no registration requirement, no geographic restriction. Our doctors are registered with the Romanian College of Physicians (CMR) and offer clinical consultations in Romanian — and in other languages including english— by secure video call, same day, seven days a week, from anywhere in Romania.</p><p>Already registered with a Romanian family doctor (medic de familie)? This service works alongside your existing care — for acute issues, second opinions, or situations where you need to speak to a doctor today rather than waiting for your next available appointment.</p><h2>What We Can Help With</h2><p>Our doctors assess and manage a wide range of acute and non-urgent medical concerns, including:</p><ul><li>Respiratory &amp; ENT</li><li>Cold, flu, sinusitis, and bronchitis</li><li>COVID-19 symptoms and post-COVID concerns</li><li>Sore throat, tonsillitis, and ear infections</li><li>Chest infections and persistent cough</li><li>Mild asthma flares</li><li>Infections</li><li>Urinary tract infections — assessment and antibiotic prescription where clinically indicated</li><li>Skin infections</li><li>Respiratory infections requiring antibiotic assessment</li><li>Gastrointestinal infections</li><li>General &amp; Systemic</li><li>Fever in adults</li><li>Headaches and migraine</li><li>Nausea, vomiting, diarrhoea, and abdominal pain</li><li>Fatigue and general malaise</li><li>Skin &amp; Eyes</li><li>Rashes, eczema flares, and mild allergic reactions</li><li>Conjunctivitis and mild eye irritation</li><li>Musculoskeletal</li><li>Back pain, neck pain, and muscle strains</li><li>Minor musculoskeletal injuries</li><li>Other</li><li>Concerns about current medication or ongoing treatment</li><li>Medical letters and documentation — sick notes, referral letters</li><li>Travel health advice</li><li>Second opinions on diagnoses or treatment plans</li></ul><h2>What Your Consultation Includes</h2><p>Full clinical assessmentYour doctor takes a complete history of your symptoms, relevant medical background, and current medications — and conducts a structured clinical assessment by video, to the same standard as an in-person consultation.</p><p>Clear explanation in plain EnglishYou leave your consultation knowing exactly what is happening, what to do next, and when to seek further care. Our doctors take time to answer your questions — no rushed appointment, no language barrier.</p><p>Electronic prescription — where clinically appropriateWhen clinically indicated, your doctor issues an electronic prescription validated in the Romanian Electronic Prescription System (SIPE). You present this at any pharmacy of your choice in Romania. For foreign citizens without a Romanian CNP, your passport number is used — no Romanian health insurance required.</p><p>Antibiotic prescription — where clinically indicatedSince 2024, all antibiotics in Romania require a doctor's prescription. Your doctor assesses your symptoms and prescribes the appropriate antibiotic where clinically justified — including for urinary tract infections, respiratory infections, and skin infections.</p><p>Referral letters and medical documentationWhen specialist referral, blood tests, imaging, or other clinical documentation is needed, your doctor issues this on the same day.</p><p>Sick notesIf you are employed in Romania and require a sick leave certificate (concediu medical), your doctor can issue the appropriate documentation where clinically indicated. The decision to issue sick leave is always a clinical decision made by the doctor after full assessment.</p><h2>A Note on Healthcare in Romania for Expats</h2><p>Romania's public health system is structurally sound but access as a foreigner involves several practical challenges:</p><p>Registration: Access to the Romanian public health system requires registration with a family doctor (medic de familie) and enrolment with a health insurance fund. This takes time and requires documentation.</p><p>Language: While many private clinic doctors in Bucharest speak English, English-speaking primary care is rare outside the capital — and conducting a meaningful medical consultation in a language you do not speak well is genuinely difficult.</p><p>Geography: English-language private clinics are concentrated in Bucharest, Cluj-Napoca, and Timișoara. For expats and international residents elsewhere in Romania, access is significantly more difficult.</p><p>Global Health removes all three barriers. CMR-registered doctors, consulting in English, available from anywhere in Romania, same day, without prior registration.</p><h2>Who This Service Is For</h2><ul><li>Expats and foreigners living in Romania without a registered Romanian family doctor</li><li>International students at Romanian universities</li><li>Digital nomads and remote workers based in Romania</li><li>Business travellers in Romania</li><li>Tourists and short-stay visitors needing same-day medical advice</li><li>Long-term residents who need to see a doctor today rather than waiting for a clinic appointment</li><li>Anyone who prefers to consult in English rather than Romanian</li></ul>
